1: import sys 
2: import hashlib 
3: from PySide6.QtWidgets import ( 
4:     QApplication, 
5:     QWidget, 
6:     QVBoxLayout, 
7:     QLabel, 
8:     QLineEdit, 
9:     QPushButton, 
10:     QMessageBox, 
11: ) 
12: SECRET = "dupadupa" 
13: def generate_license(user_id: str) -> str: 
14:     raw = user_id + SECRET 
15:     hash_hex = hashlib.sha256(raw.encode()).hexdigest() 
16:     digits = ''.join(filter(str.isdigit, hash_hex)) 
17:     return digits[:8] 
18: class KeygenWindow(QWidget): 
19:     def __init__(self): 
20:         super().__init__() 
21:         self.setWindowTitle("Generator kluczy") 
22:         self.setFixedSize(300, 180) 
23:         layout = QVBoxLayout(self) 
24:         self.label = QLabel("Podaj ID użytkownika:") 
25:         layout.addWidget(self.label) 
26:         self.input = QLineEdit() 
27:         layout.addWidget(self.input) 
28:         self.button = QPushButton("Generuj klucz") 
29:         self.button.clicked.connect(self.generate) 
30:         layout.addWidget(self.button) 
31:         self.result_label = QLabel("Klucz: -") 
32:         self.result_label.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; font-size: 14px;") 
33:         layout.addWidget(self.result_label) 
34:     def generate(self): 
35:         user_id = self.input.text().strip() 
36:         if not user_id: 
37:             QMessageBox.warning(self, "Błąd", "Podaj ID użytkownika") 
38:             return 
39:         key = generate_license(user_id) 
40:         self.result_label.setText(f"Klucz: {key}")

1: import calendar 
2: from datetime import datetime 
3: from openpyxl import Workbook 
4: from openpyxl.styles import Alignment, Font, PatternFill, Border, Side 
5: from openpyxl.utils import get_column_letter 
6: def _format_hour(time_str): 
7:     if not time_str: 
8:         return "" 
9:     if str(time_str).endswith(":00"): 
10:         return str(int(str(time_str).split(":")[0])) 
11:     return str(time_str) 
12: def export_schedule_to_excel(schedule, year, month, path): 
13:     wb = Workbook() 
14:     ws = wb.active 
15:     ws.title = "Grafik" 
16:     days_in_month = calendar.monthrange(year, month)[1] 
17:     weekdays = ["Pn", "Wt", "Śr", "Cz", "Pt", "S", "N"] 
18:     # Style 
19:     fill_sat = PatternFill(start_color="E1E1E1", end_color="E1E1E1", fill_type="solid") 
20:     fill_sun = PatternFill(start_color="C8C8C8", end_color="C8C8C8", fill_type="solid") 
21:     align_center = Alignment(horizontal="center", vertical="center") 
22:     border_thin = Border(left=Side(style='thin'), right=Side(style='thin'), 
23:                          top=Side(style='thin'), bottom=Side(style='thin')) 
24:     sum_col_start = days_in_month + 3 
25:     # NAGŁÓWEK GŁÓWNY (odwzorowanie ImageExporter) 
26:     ws.cell(row=1, column=1, value=f"Grafik planowany {month:02d}/{year}").font = Font(bold=True, size=14) 
27:     ws.cell(row=2, column=1, value="Komórka:").font = Font(bold=True, size=12) 
28:      
29:     ws.cell(row=1, column=sum_col_start-3, value="Wydruk wewnętrzny").font = Font(bold=True) 
30:     ws.cell(row=2, column=sum_col_start-3, value=f"Data: {datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y')}").font = Font(bold=True) 
31:     ws.cell(row=3, column=sum_col_start-3, value="Wygenerowany w Dingo") 
32:     # NAGŁÓWEK TABELI 
33:     ws.merge_cells("A4:B5") 
34:     ws.cell(row=4, column=1, value="Nazwisko i imię").alignment = align_center 
35:      
36:     ws.merge_cells(start_row=3, start_column=3, end_row=3, end_column=sum_col_start-1) 
37:     ws.cell(row=3, column=3, value="Dni miesiąca").alignment = align_center 
38:     ws.cell(row=3, column=3).font = Font(bold=True) 
39:      
40:     # Nagłówki dni 
41:     for day in range(1, days_in_month + 1): 
42:         col = day + 2 
43:         wd = calendar.weekday(year, month, day) 
44:         ws.cell(row=4, column=col, value=day).alignment = align_center 
45:         ws.cell(row=5, column=col, value=weekdays[wd]).alignment = align_center 
46:         if wd == 5: 
47:             ws.cell(row=4, column=col).fill = fill_sat 
48:             ws.cell(row=5, column=col).fill = fill_sat 
49:         elif wd == 6: 
50:             ws.cell(row=4, column=col).fill = fill_sun 
51:             ws.cell(row=5, column=col).fill = fill_sun 
52:     # NAGŁÓWKI PODSUMOWANIA (odwzorowanie ImageExporter) 
53:     sum_labels = ["Godziny", "Urlop", "L4", "Razem"] 
54:     for i, lbl in enumerate(sum_labels): 
55:         col = sum_col_start + i 
56:         ws.merge_cells(start_row=4, start_column=col, end_row=5, end_column=col) 
57:         c = ws.cell(row=4, column=col, value=lbl) 
58:         c.alignment = Alignment(textRotation=90, horizontal="center", vertical="center") 
59:         c.font = Font(bold=True) 
60:     # DANE PRACOWNIKÓW 
61:     cur_row = 6 
62:     for emp in schedule.employees: 
63:         # Scalone nazwisko 
64:         ws.merge_cells(start_row=cur_row, start_column=1, end_row=cur_row+2, end_column=1) 
65:         name_cell = ws.cell(row=cur_row, column=1, value=emp.display_name()) 
66:         name_cell.alignment = Alignment(horizontal="left", vertical="center", indent=1) 
67:         name_cell.font = Font(bold=True) 
68:         for i, lbl in enumerate(["od", "do", "h"]): 
69:             ws.cell(row=cur_row + i, column=2, value=lbl).alignment = align_center 
70:         # Dni miesiąca 
71:         for day in range(1, days_in_month + 1): 
72:             col = day + 2 
73:             ds = schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
74:             wd = calendar.weekday(year, month, day) 
75:              
76:             cells = [ws.cell(row=cur_row + i, column=col) for i in range(3)] 
77:             for c in cells: 
78:                 c.alignment = align_center 
79:                 if wd == 5: 
80:                     c.fill = fill_sat 
81:                 elif wd == 6: 
82:                     c.fill = fill_sun 
83:             if ds.is_leave: 
84:                 ws.merge_cells(start_row=cur_row, start_column=col, end_row=cur_row+2, end_column=col) 
85:                 cells[0].value = "URL" 
86:                 cells[0].font = Font(bold=True) 
87:             elif getattr(ds, "is_sick", False): 
88:                 ws.merge_cells(start_row=cur_row, start_column=col, end_row=cur_row+2, end_column=col) 
89:                 cells[0].value = "L4" 
90:                 cells[0].font = Font(bold=True) 
91:             elif not ds.is_empty(): 
92:                 cells[0].value = _format_hour(ds.start) 
93:                 cells[1].value = _format_hour(ds.end) 
94:                 cells[2].value = ds.total_as_str() 
95:         # PODSUMOWANIE W WIERSZU (na prawo) 
96:         values = [ 
97:             schedule.total_hours_for_employee(emp), 
98:             schedule.leave_hours_for_employee(emp), 
99:             schedule.sick_hours_for_employee(emp), 
100:             schedule.total_with_leave_and_sick_for_employee(emp) 
101:         ] 
102:         for i, val in enumerate(values): 
103:             col = sum_col_start + i 
104:             ws.merge_cells(start_row=cur_row, start_column=col, end_row=cur_row+2, end_column=col) 
105:             ws.cell(row=cur_row, column=col, value=val).alignment = align_center 
106:         cur_row += 3 
107:     # Krawędzie i szerokości 
108:     ws.column_dimensions["A"].width = 25 
109:     ws.column_dimensions["B"].width = 5 
110:     for col_idx in range(1, sum_col_start + 4): 
111:         col_ltr = get_column_letter(col_idx) 
112:         if col_idx > 2: ws.column_dimensions[col_ltr].width = 6 
113:         # Krawędzie tylko dla tabeli (od wiersza 3), omijamy nagłówek główny 
114:         for r_idx in range(3, cur_row): 
115:             # Omijamy puste pola koło "Dni miesiąca" 
116:             if r_idx == 3 and (col_idx < 3 or col_idx >= sum_col_start): 
117:                 continue 
118:             ws.cell(row=r_idx, column=col_idx).border = border_thin 
119:     wb.save(path)

1: import calendar 
2: from datetime import datetime 
3: from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont 
4: class ImageScheduleExporter: 
5:     def __init__(self, schedule, year, month): 
6:         self.schedule = schedule 
7:         self.year = year 
8:         self.month = month 
9:         self.days = calendar.monthrange(year, month)[1] 
10:         self.employees = schedule.employees 
11:         self.NAME_W = 220 
12:         self.LABEL_W = 40 
13:         self.CELL_W = 55 
14:         self.CELL_H = 22 
15:         self.HEADER_H = 140 
16:         self.FOOTER_H = 100 
17:         self.width = self.NAME_W + self.LABEL_W + self.days * self.CELL_W + 4 * 80 
18:         self.height = self.HEADER_H + len(self.employees) * 3 * self.CELL_H + self.FOOTER_H 
19:         self.img = Image.new("RGB", (self.width, self.height), "white") 
20:         self.draw = ImageDraw.Draw(self.img) 
21:         try: 
22:             self.font = ImageFont.truetype("arial.ttf", 14) 
23:             self.font_b = ImageFont.truetype("arial.ttf", 20)  # +2 
24:         except: 
25:             self.font = ImageFont.load_default() 
26:             self.font_b = self.font 
27:         self.GRID = (0, 0, 0) 
28:         self.SATURDAY = (225, 225, 225) 
29:         self.SUNDAY = (200, 200, 200) 
30:     def export(self, path): 
31:         self._draw_header() 
32:         self._draw_table() 
33:         self.img.save(path, "JPEG", quality=95) 
34:     # ================= HEADER ================= 
35:     def _draw_header(self): 
36:         x_base = int(self.width * 0.25) 
37:         self.draw.text( 
38:             (x_base, 20), 
39:             f"Grafik planowany {self.month:02d}/{self.year}", 
40:             fill=(0, 0, 0), 
41:             font=self.font_b 
42:         ) 
43:         self.draw.text( 
44:             (x_base + 300, 20), 
45:             f"Komórka:", 
46:             fill=(0, 0, 0), 
47:             font=self.font_b 
48:         ) 
49:         right_x = self.width - 260 
50:         self.draw.text((right_x, 10), "Wydruk wewnętrzny", fill=(0, 0, 0), font=self.font_b) 
51:         self.draw.text((right_x, 35), f"Data: {datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y')}", fill=(0, 0, 0), font=self.font_b) 
52:         self.draw.text((right_x, 60), "Wygenerowany w Dingo", fill=(0, 0, 0), font=self.font) 
53:     # ================= TABLE ================= 
54:     def _draw_table(self): 
55:         y = self.HEADER_H 
56:         start_x = self.NAME_W + self.LABEL_W 
57:         center = start_x + (self.days * self.CELL_W) // 2 
58:         self._draw_centered_text(center, y - 60, "Dni miesiąca", self.font_b) 
59:         table_bottom = self.HEADER_H + len(self.employees) * 3 * self.CELL_H 
60:         for d in range(1, self.days + 1): 
61:             x = start_x + (d - 1) * self.CELL_W 
62:             wd = calendar.weekday(self.year, self.month, d) 
63:             color = self.SUNDAY if wd == 6 else self.SATURDAY if wd == 5 else None 
64:             if color: 
65:                 self.draw.rectangle([x, y - 40, x + self.CELL_W, table_bottom], fill=color) 
66:             self.draw.rectangle([x, y - 40, x + self.CELL_W, y - 20], outline=self.GRID) 
67:             self.draw.text((x + self.CELL_W // 2, y - 30), str(d), fill=(0, 0, 0), font=self.font, anchor="mm") 
68:             self.draw.rectangle([x, y - 20, x + self.CELL_W, y], outline=self.GRID) 
69:             self.draw.text( 
70:                 (x + self.CELL_W // 2, y - 10), 
71:                 ["Pn", "Wt", "Śr", "Cz", "Pt", "S", "N"][wd], 
72:                 fill=(0, 0, 0), 
73:                 font=self.font, 
74:                 anchor="mm" 
75:             ) 
76:         summary_x = start_x + self.days * self.CELL_W 
77:         headers = ["Godziny", "Urlop", "L4", "Razem"] 
78:         for i, h in enumerate(headers): 
79:             x = summary_x + i * 80 
80:             self._draw_centered_text(x + 40, y - 40, h, self.font) 
81:         for emp in self.employees: 
82:             self._draw_employee(emp, y) 
83:             y += 3 * self.CELL_H 
84:     # ================= EMPLOYEE ================= 
85:     def _draw_employee(self, emp, y): 
86:         self.draw.rectangle([0, y, self.NAME_W, y + 3 * self.CELL_H], outline=self.GRID) 
87:         self._draw_centered_text(self.NAME_W // 2, y + self.CELL_H, emp.display_name(), self.font) 
88:         for i, txt in enumerate(["od", "do", "h"]): 
89:             self.draw.rectangle( 
90:                 [self.NAME_W, y + i * self.CELL_H, self.NAME_W + self.LABEL_W, y + (i + 1) * self.CELL_H], 
91:                 outline=self.GRID 
92:             ) 
93:             self._draw_centered_text(self.NAME_W + self.LABEL_W // 2, y + i * self.CELL_H + 5, txt, self.font) 
94:         for d in range(1, self.days + 1): 
95:             x = self.NAME_W + self.LABEL_W + (d - 1) * self.CELL_W 
96:             ds = self.schedule.get_day(emp, d) 
97:             if ds.is_leave: 
98:                 self.draw.rectangle([x, y, x + self.CELL_W, y + 3 * self.CELL_H], outline=self.GRID) 
99:                 self._draw_centered_text(x + self.CELL_W // 2, y + self.CELL_H, "URL", self.font_b) 
100:                 continue 
101:             if getattr(ds, "is_sick", False): 
102:                 self.draw.rectangle([x, y, x + self.CELL_W, y + 3 * self.CELL_H], outline=self.GRID) 
103:                 self._draw_centered_text(x + self.CELL_W // 2, y + self.CELL_H, "L4", self.font_b) 
104:                 continue 
105:             self.draw.rectangle([x, y, x + self.CELL_W, y + 3 * self.CELL_H], outline=self.GRID) 
106:             if not ds.is_empty(): 
107:                 mid_y = y + 2 * self.CELL_H 
108:                 self.draw.line([x + 10, mid_y, x + self.CELL_W - 10, mid_y], fill=self.GRID) 
109:                 self.draw.text((x + self.CELL_W // 2, y + self.CELL_H // 2), self._format_hour(ds.start), fill=(0, 0, 0), font=self.font, anchor="mm") 
110:                 self.draw.text((x + self.CELL_W // 2, y + self.CELL_H + self.CELL_H // 2), self._format_hour(ds.end), fill=(0, 0, 0), font=self.font, anchor="mm") 
111:                 self.draw.text((x + self.CELL_W // 2, y + 2 * self.CELL_H + self.CELL_H // 2), ds.total_as_str(), fill=(0, 0, 0), font=self.font, anchor="mm") 
112:         summary_x = self.NAME_W + self.LABEL_W + self.days * self.CELL_W 
113:         total = self.schedule.total_hours_for_employee(emp) 
114:         leave = self.schedule.leave_hours_for_employee(emp) 
115:         sick = self.schedule.sick_hours_for_employee(emp) 
116:         sum_all = self.schedule.total_with_leave_and_sick_for_employee(emp) 
117:         values = [total, leave, sick, sum_all] 
118:         for i, val in enumerate(values): 
119:             x = summary_x + i * 80 
120:             self.draw.rectangle([x, y, x + 80, y + 3 * self.CELL_H], outline=self.GRID) 
121:             self._draw_centered_text(x + 40, y + self.CELL_H, str(val), self.font) 
122:     # ================= UTILS ================= 
123:     def _format_hour(self, time_str): 
124:         if not time_str: 
125:             return "" 
126:         if time_str.endswith(":00"): 
127:             return str(int(time_str.split(":")[0])) 
128:         return time_str 
129:     def _draw_centered_text(self, x, y, text, font): 
130:         bbox = self.draw.textbbox((0, 0), text, font=font) 
131:         w = bbox[2] - bbox[0] 
132:         h = bbox[3] - bbox[1] 
133:         self.draw.text((x - w // 2, y - h // 2), text, fill=(0, 0, 0), font=font) 
134: def export_schedule_to_image(schedule, year, month, path): 
135:     exporter = ImageScheduleExporter(schedule, year, month) 
136:     exporter.export(path) 
137:     return True

351:             add_morning_afternoon_balance_penalty( 
352:                 model, 
353:                 x, 
354:                 employees, 
355:                 days, 
356:                 self.shop, 
357:                 self.SHIFT_OPEN, 
358:                 self.SHIFT_CLOSE, 
359:                 self.START_SHIFT_MAP, 
360:                 self.END_SHIFT_MAP 
361:             ) 
362:         ) 
363:         all_soft_violations.extend(availability_violations) 
364:         all_soft_violations.extend(night_violations) 
365:         all_soft_violations.extend( 
366:             add_work_balance_penalty( 
367:                 model, 
368:                 x, 
369:                 employees, 
370:                 trade_days, 
371:                 self.START_SHIFT_MAP, 
372:                 self.END_SHIFT_MAP 
373:             ) 
374:         ) 
375:         all_soft_violations.extend( 
376:             add_open_close_penalty( 
377:                 x, 
378:                 employees, 
379:                 trade_days, 
380:                 self.SHIFT_OPEN, 
381:                 self.SHIFT_CLOSE 
382:             ) 
383:         ) 
384:         if is_fix: 
385:             from logic.generator.fix import setup_fix_hints_and_penalties 
386:             fix_penalties = setup_fix_hints_and_penalties( 
387:                 model, 
388:                 x, 
389:                 employees, 
390:                 days, 
391:                 self.schedule, 
392:                 self.shop, 
393:                 self.ALL_SHIFTS, 
394:                 self.SHIFT_OPEN, 
395:                 self.SHIFT_CLOSE, 
396:                 self.START_SHIFT_MAP, 
397:                 self.END_SHIFT_MAP 
398:             ) 
399:             all_soft_violations.extend(fix_penalties) 
400:         build_objective(model, all_soft_violations) 
401:         solver, status = solve_model(model) 
402:         success = save_solution( 
403:             self.schedule, 
404:             self.shop, 
405:             solver, 
406:             status, 
407:             x, 
408:             employees, 
409:             trade_days, 
410:             self.SHIFT_OPEN, 
411:             self.SHIFT_CLOSE, 
412:             self.START_SHIFT_MAP, 
413:             self.END_SHIFT_MAP 
414:         ) 
415:         # SPRZĄTANIE: Przywracamy oryginalne daily_hours, żeby UI i zapisy nie świrowały 
416:         for emp in employees: 
417:             if hasattr(emp, '_orig_daily_hours'): 
418:                 object.__setattr__(emp, 'daily_hours', emp._orig_daily_hours) 
419:         return { 
420:             "status": status, 
421:             "success": success, 
422:             "conflicts": solver.NumConflicts(), 
423:             "branches": solver.NumBranches(), 
424:             "wall_time": solver.WallTime() 
425:         } 
426:      
427:     def _create_variables(self, model, employees, days): 
428:         x = {} 
429:         for e in range(len(employees)): 
430:             for d in days: 
431:                 for s in (self.ALL_SHIFTS): 
432:                     x[e, d, s] = model.NewBoolVar(f"x_e{e}_d{d}_s{s}") 
433:         print(f"[MODEL] variables: {len(x)}") 
434:         return x 
435:      
436:     def _apply_policy( 
437:         self, 
438:         policy_name, 
439:         hard_fn, 
440:         soft_fn=None 
441:     ): 
442:         from model.constraint_policy import ConstraintPolicy 
443:         policy = self.shop.constraint_policies.get(policy_name) 
444:         weight = self.constraint_weights.get(policy_name, 1) 
445:         print(f"[POLICY] {policy_name} -> {policy}") 
446:         if policy == ConstraintPolicy.MANDATORY: 
447:             print(f"[HARD] {policy_name}") 
448:             hard_fn() 
449:             return [] 
450:         elif policy == ConstraintPolicy.PREFERRED: 
451:             print(f"[SOFT] {policy_name} weight={weight}") 
452:             if soft_fn: 
453:                 violations = soft_fn() 
454:                 # zamieniamy violation vars na weighted terms 
455:                 return [weight * v for v in violations] 
456:             else: 
457:                 hard_fn() 
458:                 return [] 
459:         elif policy == ConstraintPolicy.DISABLED: 
460:             return [] 
461:         return []

1: class ScheduleController: 
2:     def __init__(self, schedule, shop_config): 
3:         self.schedule = schedule 
4:         self.shop_config = shop_config 
5:         self.history = [] 
6:         self.future = [] 
7:     def snapshot(self): 
8:         self.history.append(self.schedule.snapshot()) 
9:         self.future.clear() 
10:     def undo(self): 
11:         if not self.history: 
12:             return self.schedule 
13:         self.future.append(self.schedule.snapshot()) 
14:         self.schedule = self.history.pop() 
15:         return self.schedule 
16:     def set_day_free(self, emp, day): 
17:         ds = self.schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
18:         if ds.start is None and ds.end is None and not ds.is_leave and not ds.is_sick: 
19:             return 
20:         self.snapshot() 
21:         self.schedule.set_day_free(emp, day) 
22:         ds.is_locked = True 
23:     def set_day_hours(self, emp, day, start, end): 
24:         from datetime import datetime 
25:         fmt = "%H:%M" 
26:         try: 
27:             start_dt = datetime.strptime(start, fmt) 
28:             end_dt = datetime.strptime(end, fmt) 
29:         except: 
30:             return  # nieprawidłowy format → ignoruj 
31:         # ❌ BLOKADA: koniec <= start 
32:         if end_dt <= start_dt: 
33:             return 
34:         ds = self.schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
35:         if ds.start == start and ds.end == end and not ds.is_leave and not ds.is_sick: 
36:             return 
37:         self.snapshot() 
38:         self.schedule.set_day_hours(emp, day, start, end) 
39:         ds.is_locked = True 
40:     def set_day_leave(self, emp, day): 
41:         ds = self.schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
42:         if ds.is_leave: 
43:             return 
44:         self.snapshot() 
45:         ds.set_leave() 
46:         ds.is_locked = True 
47:     def add_employee(self, emp): 
48:         self.snapshot() 
49:         self.schedule.add_employee(emp) 
50:     def replace_employee(self, old, new): 
51:         self.snapshot() 
52:         self.schedule.replace_employee(old, new) 
53:     def get_day(self, emp, day): 
54:         return self.schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
55:     def generate_schedule(self, force=False): 
56:         from logic.auto_generator import AutoScheduleGenerator 
57:         generator = AutoScheduleGenerator(self.schedule, self.shop_config) 
58:          
59:         is_fix = getattr(self.schedule, "is_generated", False) and not force 
60:         result = generator.generate(is_fix=is_fix) 
61:          
62:         if result and result.get("success"): 
63:             self.schedule.is_generated = True 
64:              
65:         return result 
66:     def set_shift(self, emp, day, shift_type, start=None, end=None): 
67:         ds = self.schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
68:         # --- BLOKADA DUPLIKATÓW --- 
69:         if shift_type == "OFF": 
70:             if ds.start is None and ds.end is None and not ds.is_leave and not ds.is_sick: 
71:                 return 
72:         elif shift_type == "LEAVE": 
73:             if ds.is_leave: 
74:                 return 
75:         elif shift_type == "WORK": 
76:             if start is not None and end is not None: 
77:                 if ds.start == start and ds.end == end and not ds.is_leave and not ds.is_sick: 
78:                     return 
79:         self.snapshot() 
80:         if shift_type == "OFF": 
81:             ds.start = None 
82:             ds.end = None 
83:             ds.is_leave = False 
84:             ds.is_sick = False 
85:         elif shift_type == "LEAVE": 
86:             ds.start = None 
87:             ds.end = None 
88:             ds.is_leave = True 
89:             ds.is_sick = False 
90:         elif shift_type == "WORK": 
91:             from datetime import datetime 
92:             fmt = "%H:%M" 
93:             if start is not None and end is not None: 
94:                 try: 
95:                     start_dt = datetime.strptime(start, fmt) 
96:                     end_dt = datetime.strptime(end, fmt) 
97:                 except: 
98:                     return 
99:                 if end_dt <= start_dt: 
100:                     return 
101:                 ds.start = start 
102:                 ds.end = end 
103:             else: 
104:                 hours = self.shop_config.get_open_hours_for_day(day) 
105:                 if hours: 
106:                     ds.start, ds.end = hours 
107:             ds.is_leave = False 
108:             ds.is_sick = False 
109:         ds.is_locked = True 
110:     def _calc_end_from_daily(self, start_str, hours): 
111:         from datetime import datetime, timedelta 
112:         fmt = "%H:%M" 
113:         start = datetime.strptime(start_str, fmt) 
114:         end = start + timedelta(hours=hours) 
115:         return end.strftime(fmt) 
116:      
117:     def remove_employee(self, employee): 
118:         self.schedule.remove_employee(employee) 
119:     def set_day_sick(self, emp, day): 
120:         ds = self.schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
121:         if ds.is_sick: 
122:             return 
123:         self.snapshot() 
124:         ds.set_sick() 
125:         ds.is_locked = True 
126:     def redo(self): 
127:         if not self.future: 
128:             return self.schedule 
129:         self.history.append(self.schedule.snapshot()) 
130:         self.schedule = self.future.pop() 
131:         return self.schedule

4: class CellView:

6:     text_end: str = ""

11:     def __init__(self, schedule, shop_config): 
12:         self.schedule = schedule 
13:         self.shop_config = shop_config 
14:     def get_cell_view(self, emp, day) -> CellView: 
15:         ds = self.schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
16:         s, e, t = ds.as_rows() 
17:         if ds.is_leave: 
18:             return CellView( 
19:                 text_start="🌴", 
20:                 text_end="", 
21:                 text_total="", 
22:                 bg=theme.OK_GREEN, 
23:                 tooltip="Urlop", 
24:             ) 
25:         if not self.shop_config.is_trade_day(day): 
26:             return CellView(bg=theme.BG_DISABLED) 
27:         if not s or not e: 
28:             return CellView(bg=theme.BG_MAIN) 
29:         hours = self.shop_config.get_open_hours_for_day(day) 
30:         text_start = s 
31:         text_end = e 
32:         bg = theme.BG_MAIN 
33:         if hours: 
34:             open_t, close_t = hours 
35:             if s == open_t and emp.daily_hours == 8: 
36:                 text_start = "OTW" 
37:                 text_end = "" 
38:             elif e == close_t and emp.daily_hours == 8: 
39:                 text_start = "ZAM" 
40:                 text_end = "" 
41:             if s == open_t: 
42:                 bg = theme.SHIFT_MORNING 
43:             elif e == close_t: 
44:                 bg = theme.SHIFT_CLOSE 
45:         tooltip = f"{s} - {e}\nSuma: {t}" 
46:         return CellView( 
47:             text_start=text_start, 
48:             text_end=text_end, 
49:             text_total=t, 
50:             bg=bg, 
51:             tooltip=tooltip, 
52:         )

1: from logic.generator.availability_mapper import get_allowed_shifts_for_day 
2: from logic.utils.time_utils import get_effective_daily_hours 
3: def add_availability_constraint( 
4:     model, 
5:     x, 
6:     employees, 
7:     days, 
8:     shop, 
9:     all_shifts, 
10:     SHIFT_OPEN, 
11:     SHIFT_CLOSE, 
12:     START_SHIFT_MAP, 
13:     END_SHIFT_MAP, 
14:     soft=False 
15: ): 
16:     violations = [] 
17:     for e in range(len(employees)): 
18:         emp = employees[e] 
19:         eff_hours = get_effective_daily_hours(emp, shop) 
20:         for d in days: 
21:             if not shop.is_trade_day(d): 
22:                 continue 
23:             allowed = get_allowed_shifts_for_day( 
24:                 emp, 
25:                 d, 
26:                 shop, 
27:                 SHIFT_OPEN, 
28:                 SHIFT_CLOSE, 
29:                 START_SHIFT_MAP, 
30:                 END_SHIFT_MAP 
31:             ) 
32:             # brak ograniczeń → skip 
33:             if allowed is None: 
34:                 continue 
35:             allowed_set = set(allowed) 
36:             # 🔥 NOWE: odrzucamy zmiany które nie mieszczą się w dostępności przy tej długości zmiany 
37:             # (bazujemy na mapperze, ale filtrujemy realnie) 
38:             filtered_allowed = set() 
39:             for s in allowed_set: 
40:                 filtered_allowed.add(s)  # mapper już robi większość roboty 
41:             for s in all_shifts: 
42:                 if s in filtered_allowed: 
43:                     continue 
44:                 if soft: 
45:                     v = model.NewBoolVar(f"avail_violation_e{e}_d{d}_s{s}") 
46:                     model.Add(x[e, d, s] == 1).OnlyEnforceIf(v) 
47:                     violations.append(v) 
48:                 else: 
49:                     model.Add(x[e, d, s] == 0) 
50:     return violations

1: from datetime import datetime, timedelta 
2: def get_effective_daily_hours(emp, shop): 
3:     hours = shop.standard_daily_hours * emp.employment_fraction 
4:     minutes = int(hours * 60) 
5:     minutes = (minutes // 15) * 15 
6:     return minutes / 60 
7: def get_allowed_shifts_for_day( 
8:     emp, 
9:     day, 
10:     shop, 
11:     SHIFT_OPEN, 
12:     SHIFT_CLOSE, 
13:     START_SHIFT_MAP, 
14:     END_SHIFT_MAP 
15: ): 
16:     availability = getattr(emp, "availability", {}) 
17:     weekday = shop.weekday(day) 
18:     rules = availability.get(weekday) 
19:     # brak preferencji → wszystko dozwolone 
20:     if not rules: 
21:         return None 
22:     hours = shop.get_open_hours_for_day(day) 
23:     if not hours: 
24:         return [] 
25:     open_time, close_time = hours 
26:     fmt = "%H:%M" 
27:     open_dt = datetime.strptime(open_time, fmt) 
28:     close_dt = datetime.strptime(close_time, fmt) 
29:     eff_hours = get_effective_daily_hours(emp, shop) 
30:     shift_delta = timedelta(hours=eff_hours) 
31:     allowed = [] 
32:     for rule in rules: 
33:         start_pref = rule["start"] 
34:         end_pref = rule["end"] 
35:         pref_start = datetime.strptime(start_pref, fmt) 
36:         pref_end = datetime.strptime(end_pref, fmt) 
37:         # ===== OPEN ===== 
38:         start = open_dt 
39:         end = start + shift_delta 
40:         if pref_start <= start and end <= pref_end: 
41:             allowed.append(SHIFT_OPEN) 
42:         # ===== CLOSE ===== 
43:         end = close_dt 
44:         start = end - shift_delta 
45:         if pref_start <= start and end <= pref_end: 
46:             allowed.append(SHIFT_CLOSE) 
47:         # ===== START SHIFTS ===== 
48:         for shift, offset in START_SHIFT_MAP.items(): 
49:             start = open_dt + timedelta(minutes=offset) 
50:             end = start + shift_delta 
51:             if pref_start <= start and end <= pref_end: 
52:                 allowed.append(shift) 
53:         # ===== END SHIFTS ===== 
54:         for shift, offset in END_SHIFT_MAP.items(): 
55:             end = close_dt - timedelta(minutes=offset) 
56:             start = end - shift_delta 
57:             if pref_start <= start and end <= pref_end: 
58:                 allowed.append(shift) 
59:     return list(set(allowed))

1: def add_one_shift_per_day_constraint(model, x, employees, days, all_shifts): 
2:     for e in range(len(employees)): 
3:         for d in days: 
4:             model.Add( 
5:                 sum(x[e, d, s] for s in all_shifts) <= 1 
6:             )

8:     for e in range(len(employees)): 
9:         for d in days: 
10:             if not shop.is_trade_day(d): 
11:                 for s in all_shifts: 
12:                     model.Add(x[e, d, s] == 0)

15:         emp = employees[e] 
16:         for d in days: 
17:             day_state = schedule.get_day(emp, d) 
18:             if day_state.is_leave or day_state.is_sick: 
19:                 for s in all_shifts: 
20:                     model.Add(x[e, d, s] == 0)

22:                 )

1: def add_fixed_staff_shift_constraints( 
2:     model, 
3:     x, 
4:     employees, 
5:     trade_days, 
6:     shift_type, 
7:     min_staff, 
8:     soft=False 
9: ): 
10:     print(f"[CONSTRAINT] fixed_staff shift={shift_type} min={min_staff} soft={soft}") 
11:     violations = [] 
12:     for d in trade_days: 
13:         total_staff = sum( 
14:             x[e, d, shift_type] for e in range(len(employees)) 
15:         ) 
16:         opener_staff = sum( 
17:             x[e, d, shift_type] 
18:             for e in range(len(employees)) 
19:             if employees[e].is_opener 
20:         ) 
21:         if not soft: 
22:             meat_staff = sum( 
23:                 x[e, d, shift_type] 
24:                 for e in range(len(employees)) 
25:                 if employees[e].is_meat 
26:             ) 
27:             # Twarde ograniczenia: suma osób musi się zgadzać,  
28:             # ale składy specjalistyczne mogą się nakładać. 
29:             model.Add(total_staff == min_staff) 
30:             model.Add(opener_staff >= 1) 
31:             model.Add(meat_staff >= 1) 
32:         else: 
33:             # Logika soft (kary) jest już poprawnie oparta na >= 1 
34:             total_violation = model.NewIntVar( 
35:                 0, min_staff, 
36:                 f"staff_v_d{d}_{shift_type}" 
37:             ) 
38:             model.Add(total_staff + total_violation >= min_staff) 
39:             violations.append(total_violation) 
40:             opener_violation = model.NewBoolVar( 
41:                 f"opener_v_d{d}_{shift_type}" 
42:             ) 
43:             model.Add(opener_staff + opener_violation >= 1) 
44:             violations.append(opener_violation) 
45:             # Dodajmy mięso do wersji soft, skoro go brakowało 
46:             meat_staff = sum( 
47:                 x[e, d, shift_type] 
48:                 for e in range(len(employees)) 
49:                 if employees[e].is_meat 
50:             ) 
51:             meat_violation = model.NewBoolVar(f"meat_v_d{d}_{shift_type}") 
52:             model.Add(meat_staff + meat_violation >= 1) 
53:             violations.append(meat_violation) 
54:     return violations 
55: def add_max_consecutive_constraint( 
56:     model, 
57:     x, 
58:     employees, 
59:     days, 
60:     max_consecutive, 
61:     all_shifts, 
62:     soft=False 
63: ): 
64:     violations = [] 
65:     for e in range(len(employees)): 
66:         for start in range(1, len(days) - max_consecutive + 1): 
67:             work_sum = sum( 
68:                 x[e, d, s] 
69:                 for d in range(start, start + max_consecutive + 1) 
70:                 for s in all_shifts 
71:             ) 
72:             if not soft: 
73:                 model.Add(work_sum <= max_consecutive) 
74:             else: 
75:                 violation = model.NewIntVar( 
76:                     0, len(days), 
77:                     f"max_consec_violation_e{e}_d{start}" 
78:                 ) 
79:                 model.Add(work_sum <= max_consecutive + violation) 
80:                 violations.append(violation) 
81:     return violations

1: from logic.generator.manual_constraint import resolve_manual_shift 
2: import datetime 
3: def get_iso_week(year, month, day): 
4:     return datetime.date(year, month, day).isocalendar()[1] 
5: def setup_fix_hints_and_penalties( 
6:     model, 
7:     x, 
8:     employees, 
9:     days, 
10:     schedule, 
11:     shop, 
12:     all_shifts, 
13:     SHIFT_OPEN, 
14:     SHIFT_CLOSE, 
15:     START_SHIFT_MAP, 
16:     END_SHIFT_MAP 
17: ): 
18:     """ 
19:     FIX MODE — stabilna wersja: 
20:     - HARD blokuje rzeczy krytyczne (OPEN/CLOSE, manuale) 
21:     - kontroluje nominal (z marginesem) 
22:     - ogranicza ilość hintów (żeby solver się nie wieszał) 
23:     """ 
24:     penalties = [] 
25:     # ================================================== 
26:     # 🔒 HARD: popo → brak OPEN następnego dnia 
27:     # ================================================== 
28:     afternoon_shifts = [SHIFT_CLOSE] + list(END_SHIFT_MAP.keys()) 
29:     for e in range(len(employees)): 
30:         for d in days[:-1]: 
31:             worked_afternoon = model.NewBoolVar(f"aft_e{e}_d{d}") 
32:             model.Add( 
33:                 sum(x[e, d, s] for s in afternoon_shifts) >= 1 
34:             ).OnlyEnforceIf(worked_afternoon) 
35:             model.Add( 
36:                 sum(x[e, d, s] for s in afternoon_shifts) == 0 
37:             ).OnlyEnforceIf(worked_afternoon.Not()) 
38:             # 🔥 HARD 
39:             model.Add(x[e, d + 1, SHIFT_OPEN] == 0).OnlyEnforceIf(worked_afternoon) 
40:     # ================================================== 
41:     # 📅 znajdź edytowane tygodnie 
42:     # ================================================== 
43:     edited_weeks = set() 
44:     for emp in employees: 
45:         for d in days: 
46:             day_state = schedule.get_day(emp, d) 
47:             if day_state.is_locked: 
48:                 week_num = get_iso_week(schedule.year, schedule.month, d) 
49:                 edited_weeks.add(week_num) 
50:     # ================================================== 
51:     # ⏱️ NOMINAL (ważne — ale nie HARD) 
52:     # ================================================== 
53:     from logic.utils.time_utils import get_effective_daily_hours 
54:     nominal_hours = shop.get_full_time_nominal_hours() 
55:     nominal_minutes = nominal_hours * 60 
56:     for e, emp in enumerate(employees): 
57:         shift_minutes = int(get_effective_daily_hours(emp, shop) * 60) 
58:         leave_days = 0 
59:         sick_days = 0 
60:         for d in days: 
61:             ds = schedule.get_day(emp, d) 
62:             if ds.is_leave: 
63:                 leave_days += 1 
64:             if getattr(ds, "is_sick", False): 
65:                 sick_days += 1 
66:         daily_hours = get_effective_daily_hours(emp, shop) 
67:         leave_minutes = int(leave_days * daily_hours * 60) 
68:         sick_minutes = int(sick_days * daily_hours * 60) 
69:         target_minutes = int( 
70:             nominal_minutes * emp.employment_fraction 
71:             - leave_minutes 
72:             - sick_minutes 
73:         ) 
74:         total_worked = model.NewIntVar(0, 50000, f"fix_total_e{e}") 
75:         model.Add( 
76:             total_worked == 
77:             sum( 
78:                 x[e, d, s] * shift_minutes 
79:                 for d in days 
80:                 for s in all_shifts 
81:                 if s = 14 
82:             ) 
83:         ) 
84:         # 🔥 TWARDY LIMIT (mały margines) 
85:         max_over = int(emp.daily_hours * 60) 
86:         model.Add(total_worked <= target_minutes + max_over) 
87:         # 🔥 SOFT niedobór (mocny) 
88:         under = model.NewIntVar(0, 50000, f"fix_under_e{e}") 
89:         model.Add(total_worked + under >= target_minutes) 
90:         penalties.append(under * 4000) 
91:     # ================================================== 
92:     # 🔄 SHIFT MATCHING (LEKKIE, NIE AGRESYWNE) 
93:     # ================================================== 
94:     start_map_rev = {v: k for k, v in START_SHIFT_MAP.items()} 
95:     end_map_rev = {v: k for k, v in END_SHIFT_MAP.items()} 
96:     for e, emp in enumerate(employees): 
97:         for d in days: 
98:             day_state = schedule.get_day(emp, d) 
99:             # ================================================== 
100:             # 🔒 HARD: urlop / sick 
101:             # ================================================== 
102:             if day_state.is_leave or getattr(day_state, "is_sick", False): 
103:                 for s in all_shifts: 
104:                     model.Add(x[e, d, s] == 0) 
105:                 continue 
106:             # ================================================== 
107:             # 🔒 HARD: locked → NIE RUSZAJ 
108:             # ================================================== 
109:             if day_state.is_locked: 
110:                 continue 
111:             week_num = get_iso_week(schedule.year, schedule.month, d) 
112:             is_edited_week = week_num in edited_weeks 
113:             weight = 300 if is_edited_week else 1200 
114:             # ================================================== 
115:             # 🧠 rozpoznanie obecnej zmiany 
116:             # ================================================== 
117:             current_shift = None 
118:             start = getattr(day_state, "start", None) 
119:             end = getattr(day_state, "end", None) 
120:             if start and end: 
121:                 hours = shop.get_open_hours_for_day(d) 
122:                 if hours: 
123:                     open_time, close_time = hours 
124:                     shift_name = resolve_manual_shift( 
125:                         start, 
126:                         end, 
127:                         open_time, 
128:                         close_time, 
129:                         start_map_rev, 
130:                         end_map_rev 
131:                     ) 
132:                     if shift_name == "OPEN": 
133:                         current_shift = SHIFT_OPEN 
134:                     elif shift_name == "CLOSE": 
135:                         current_shift = SHIFT_CLOSE 
136:                     elif shift_name is not None: 
137:                         current_shift = shift_name 
138:             # ================================================== 
139:             # ⚠️ tylko JEDEN hint (ważne) 
140:             # ================================================== 
141:             if current_shift is not None: 
142:                 model.AddHint(x[e, d, current_shift], 1) 
143:                 penalties.append(weight * (1 - x[e, d, current_shift])) 
144:                 # 🔥 OPEN/CLOSE ważniejsze 
145:                 if current_shift in (SHIFT_OPEN, SHIFT_CLOSE): 
146:                     penalties.append(2000 * (1 - x[e, d, current_shift])) 
147:     return penalties

1: from logic.utils.time_utils import get_effective_daily_hours 
2: def add_monthly_hours_constraint( 
3:     model, 
4:     x, 
5:     employees, 
6:     days, 
7:     schedule, 
8:     shop, 
9:     all_shifts, 
10:     soft=False 
11: ): 
12:     violations = [] 
13:     nominal_hours = shop.get_full_time_nominal_hours() 
14:     nominal_minutes = nominal_hours * 60 
15:     all_totals = [] 
16:     for e in range(len(employees)): 
17:         emp = employees[e] 
18:         shift_minutes = int(get_effective_daily_hours(emp, shop) * 60) 
19:         leave_days = 0 
20:         sick_days = 0 
21:         for d in days: 
22:             ds = schedule.get_day(emp, d) 
23:             if ds.is_leave: 
24:                 leave_days += 1 
25:             if getattr(ds, "is_sick", False): 
26:                 sick_days += 1 
27:         daily_hours = get_effective_daily_hours(emp, shop) 
28:         leave_minutes = int(leave_days * daily_hours * 60) 
29:         sick_minutes = int(sick_days * daily_hours * 60) 
30:         total_minutes = model.NewIntVar(0, 50000, f"month_total_e{e}") 
31:         model.Add( 
32:             total_minutes == 
33:             sum( 
34:                 x[e, d, s] * shift_minutes 
35:                 for d in days 
36:                 for s in all_shifts 
37:                 if s = 14 
38:             ) 
39:         ) 
40:         all_totals.append(total_minutes) 
41:         target_minutes = int(nominal_minutes * emp.employment_fraction - leave_minutes - sick_minutes) 
42:         if not soft: 
43:             model.Add(total_minutes >= target_minutes) 
44:             model.Add(total_minutes <= target_minutes + (emp.daily_hours * 60)) 
45:         else: 
46:             under = model.NewIntVar(0, 50000, f"under_e{e}") 
47:             model.Add(total_minutes + under >= target_minutes) 
48:             over = model.NewIntVar(0, 50000, f"over_e{e}") 
49:             model.Add(total_minutes <= target_minutes + over) 
50:             violations.append(under) 
51:             violations.append(over) 
52:     if soft and len(all_totals) > 1: 
53:         max_total = model.NewIntVar(0, 50000, "month_max_total") 
54:         min_total = model.NewIntVar(0, 50000, "month_min_total") 
55:         model.AddMaxEquality(max_total, all_totals) 
56:         model.AddMinEquality(min_total, all_totals) 
57:         spread = model.NewIntVar(0, 50000, "month_spread") 
58:         model.Add(spread == max_total - min_total) 
59:         violations.append(spread) 
60:     return violations 
61: def add_balance_constraint( 
62:     model, 
63:     x, 
64:     employees, 
65:     days, 
66:     shop, 
67:     all_shifts, 
68:     soft=True 
69: ): 
70:     nominal = shop.get_full_time_nominal_hours() 
71:     if not nominal: 
72:         return [] 
73:     violations = [] 
74:     for e in range(len(employees)): 
75:         emp = employees[e] 
76:         nominal_minutes = int(nominal * 60 * emp.employment_fraction) 
77:         shift_minutes = int(get_effective_daily_hours(emp, shop) * 60) 
78:         total_minutes = model.NewIntVar(0, 20000, f"total_minutes_e{e}") 
79:         model.Add( 
80:             total_minutes == 
81:             sum( 
82:                 sum(x[e, d, s] for s in all_shifts) * shift_minutes 
83:                 for d in days 
84:             ) 
85:         ) 
86:         if not soft: 
87:             model.Add(total_minutes == nominal_minutes) 
88:         else: 
89:             diff = model.NewIntVar(-20000, 20000, f"diff_e{e}") 
90:             model.Add(diff == total_minutes - nominal_minutes) 
91:             abs_diff = model.NewIntVar(0, 20000, f"abs_diff_e{e}") 
92:             model.AddAbsEquality(abs_diff, diff) 
93:             violations.append(abs_diff) 
94:     return violations

1: from datetime import datetime 
2: def resolve_manual_shift( 
3:     start, 
4:     end, 
5:     open_time, 
6:     close_time, 
7:     start_map, 
8:     end_map 
9: ): 
10:     fmt = "%H:%M" 
11:     if start == open_time: 
12:         return "OPEN" 
13:     if end == close_time: 
14:         return "CLOSE" 
15:     open_dt = datetime.strptime(open_time, fmt) 
16:     start_dt = datetime.strptime(start, fmt) 
17:     diff = int((start_dt - open_dt).total_seconds() / 60) 
18:     if diff in start_map: 
19:         return start_map[diff] 
20:     close_dt = datetime.strptime(close_time, fmt) 
21:     end_dt = datetime.strptime(end, fmt) 
22:     diff = int((close_dt - end_dt).total_seconds() / 60) 
23:     if diff in end_map: 
24:         return end_map[diff] 
25:     return None 
26: def add_manual_shift_constraints( 
27:     model, 
28:     x, 
29:     employees, 
30:     days, 
31:     schedule, 
32:     shop, 
33:     all_shifts, 
34:     SHIFT_OPEN, 
35:     SHIFT_CLOSE, 
36:     START_SHIFT_MAP, 
37:     END_SHIFT_MAP 
38: ): 
39:     for e in range(len(employees)): 
40:         emp = employees[e] 
41:         for d in days: 
42:             day_state = schedule.get_day(emp, d) 
43:             # 🔴 URLop 
44:             if day_state.is_leave: 
45:                 for s in all_shifts: 
46:                     model.Add(x[e, d, s] == 0) 
47:                 continue 
48:             if not day_state.is_locked: 
49:                 continue 
50:             start = getattr(day_state, "start", None) 
51:             end = getattr(day_state, "end", None) 
52:             # 🔴 wolne 
53:             if not start or not end: 
54:                 for s in all_shifts: 
55:                     model.Add(x[e, d, s] == 0) 
56:                 continue 
57:             hours = shop.get_open_hours_for_day(d) 
58:             if not hours: 
59:                 continue 
60:             open_time, close_time = hours 
61:             shift = resolve_manual_shift( 
62:                 start, 
63:                 end, 
64:                 open_time, 
65:                 close_time, 
66:                 {v: k for k, v in START_SHIFT_MAP.items()}, 
67:                 {v: k for k, v in END_SHIFT_MAP.items()} 
68:             ) 
69:             if shift == "OPEN": 
70:                 shift = SHIFT_OPEN 
71:             elif shift == "CLOSE": 
72:                 shift = SHIFT_CLOSE 
73:             if shift is None: 
74:                 continue 
75:             # 🔥 HARD LOCK — solver MUSI to ustawić 
76:             model.Add(x[e, d, shift] == 1) 
77:             for s in all_shifts: 
78:                 if s  
79:                     model.Add(x[e, d, s] == 0)

1: from datetime import datetime, timedelta 
2: from logic.utils.time_utils import get_effective_daily_hours 
3: def add_meat_constraint( 
4:     model, 
5:     x, 
6:     employees, 
7:     days, 
8:     trade_days, 
9:     all_shifts, 
10:     SHIFT_OPEN, 
11:     SHIFT_CLOSE, 
12:     soft=False 
13: ): 
14:     violations = [] 
15:     for d in trade_days: 
16:         meat_on_open = sum( 
17:             x[e, d, SHIFT_OPEN] 
18:             for e in range(len(employees)) 
19:             if employees[e].is_meat 
20:         ) 
21:         meat_on_close = sum( 
22:             x[e, d, SHIFT_CLOSE] 
23:             for e in range(len(employees)) 
24:             if employees[e].is_meat 
25:         ) 
26:         meat_on_work = sum( 
27:             x[e, d, s] 
28:             for e in range(len(employees)) 
29:             for s in all_shifts 
30:             if s not in (SHIFT_OPEN, SHIFT_CLOSE) 
31:             and employees[e].is_meat 
32:         ) 
33:         total_work = sum( 
34:             x[e, d, s] 
35:             for e in range(len(employees)) 
36:             for s in all_shifts 
37:             if s not in (SHIFT_OPEN, SHIFT_CLOSE) 
38:         ) 
39:         if not soft: 
40:             work_exists = model.NewBoolVar(f"work_exists_d{d}") 
41:             model.Add(total_work >= 1).OnlyEnforceIf(work_exists) 
42:             model.Add(total_work == 0).OnlyEnforceIf(work_exists.Not()) 
43:             model.Add( 
44:                 meat_on_work >= 1 
45:             ).OnlyEnforceIf(work_exists) 
46:         else: 
47:             v1 = model.NewBoolVar(f"meat_open_v_d{d}") 
48:             model.Add(meat_on_open + v1 >= 1) 
49:             violations.append(v1) 
50:             v2 = model.NewBoolVar(f"meat_close_v_d{d}") 
51:             model.Add(meat_on_close + v2 >= 1) 
52:             violations.append(v2) 
53:     return violations 
54: def add_meat_coverage_constraint( 
55:     model, 
56:     x, 
57:     employees, 
58:     trade_days, 
59:     shop, 
60:     SHIFT_OPEN, 
61:     SHIFT_CLOSE, 
62:     START_SHIFT_MAP, 
63:     END_SHIFT_MAP, 
64:     soft=False 
65: ): 
66:     violations = [] 
67:     for d in trade_days: 
68:         hours = shop.get_open_hours_for_day(d) 
69:         if not hours: 
70:             continue 
71:         open_time, close_time = hours 
72:         fmt = "%H:%M" 
73:         open_dt = datetime.strptime(open_time, fmt) 
74:         close_dt = datetime.strptime(close_time, fmt) 
75:         t = open_dt 
76:         while t < close_dt: 
77:             slot = t.strftime(fmt) 
78:             meat_cover = [] 
79:             for e in range(len(employees)): 
80:                 if not employees[e].is_meat: 
81:                     continue 
82:                 emp = employees[e] 
83:                 shift_len = timedelta(hours=get_effective_daily_hours(emp, shop)) 
84:                 # OPEN 
85:                 start = open_dt 
86:                 end = start + shift_len 
87:                 if start <= t < end: 
88:                     meat_cover.append(x[e, d, SHIFT_OPEN]) 
89:                 # CLOSE 
90:                 end = close_dt 
91:                 start = end - shift_len 
92:                 if start <= t < end: 
93:                     meat_cover.append(x[e, d, SHIFT_CLOSE]) 
94:                 # WORK od otwarcia 
95:                 for shift, offset in START_SHIFT_MAP.items(): 
96:                     work_start = open_dt + timedelta(minutes=offset) 
97:                     work_end = work_start + shift_len 
98:                     if work_start <= t < work_end: 
99:                         meat_cover.append(x[e, d, shift]) 
100:                 # WORK od zamknięcia 
101:                 for shift, offset in END_SHIFT_MAP.items(): 
102:                     work_end = close_dt - timedelta(minutes=offset) 
103:                     work_start = work_end - shift_len 
104:                     if work_start <= t < work_end: 
105:                         meat_cover.append(x[e, d, shift]) 
106:             if not meat_cover: 
107:                 t += timedelta(minutes=15) 
108:                 continue 
109:             if not soft: 
110:                 model.Add(sum(meat_cover) >= 1) 
111:             else: 
112:                 v = model.NewBoolVar(f"meat_cover_v_d{d}_{slot}") 
113:                 model.Add(sum(meat_cover) + v >= 1) 
114:                 violations.append(v) 
115:             t += timedelta(minutes=15) 
116:     return violations

FILE: C:\Users\kewi1\Desktop\Grafik dino V2\logic\generator\night_constraint.py

1: from datetime import datetime, timedelta 
2: from logic.utils.time_utils import get_effective_daily_hours 
3: # Sprawdza, czy pracownik ma zaznaczony checkbox "Brak pracy w godziach nocnych" i nakłada ograniczenia, jeśli tak 
4: def add_no_night_constraint( 
5:     model, 
6:     x, 
7:     employees, 
8:     days, 
9:     shop, 
10:     all_shifts, 
11:     SHIFT_OPEN, 
12:     SHIFT_CLOSE, 
13:     START_SHIFT_MAP, 
14:     END_SHIFT_MAP, 
15:     soft=False 
16: ): 
17:     violations = [] 
18:     fmt = "%H:%M" 
19:     for e in range(len(employees)): 
20:         emp = employees[e] 
21:         # 🔴 tylko dla pracowników z ograniczeniem 
22:         if not getattr(emp, "no_night", False): 
23:             continue 
24:         eff_hours = get_effective_daily_hours(emp, shop) 
25:         shift_delta = timedelta(hours=eff_hours) 
26:         for d in days: 
27:             hours = shop.get_open_hours_for_day(d) 
28:             if not hours: 
29:                 continue 
30:             open_time, close_time = hours 
31:             open_dt = datetime.strptime(open_time, fmt) 
32:             close_dt = datetime.strptime(close_time, fmt) 
33:             forbidden_shifts = set() 
34:             # ===== OPEN ===== 
35:             start = open_dt 
36:             end = start + shift_delta 
37:             if end.hour >= 22 or start.hour <= 6: 
38:                 forbidden_shifts.add(SHIFT_OPEN) 
39:             # ===== CLOSE ===== 
40:             end = close_dt 
41:             if end.hour >= 22 or start.hour <= 6: 
42:                 forbidden_shifts.add(SHIFT_CLOSE) 
43:             # ===== START SHIFTS ===== 
44:             for shift, offset in START_SHIFT_MAP.items(): 
45:                 start = open_dt + timedelta(minutes=offset) 
46:                 end = start + shift_delta 
47:                 if end.hour >= 22 or start.hour <= 6: 
48:                     forbidden_shifts.add(shift) 
49:             # ===== END SHIFTS ===== 
50:             for shift, offset in END_SHIFT_MAP.items(): 
51:                 end = close_dt - timedelta(minutes=offset) 
52:                 if end.hour >= 22 or start.hour <= 6: 
53:                     forbidden_shifts.add(shift) 
54:             # ===== nakładamy constraint ===== 
55:             for s in forbidden_shifts: 
56:                 if soft: 
57:                     v = model.NewBoolVar(f"night_violation_e{e}_d{d}_s{s}") 
58:                     model.Add(x[e, d, s] == 1).OnlyEnforceIf(v) 
59:                     violations.append(v) 
60:                 else: 
61:                     model.Add(x[e, d, s] == 0) 
62:     return violations

12:     x,

14:     days, 
15:     START_SHIFT_MAP, 
16:     END_SHIFT_MAP 
17: ):

22:             for d in days

26:             x[e, d, s]

31:         model.Add(diff == work_start - work_end)

36: def add_morning_afternoon_balance_penalty( 
37:     model, 
38:     x, 
39:     employees, 
40:     days, 
41:     shop, # Zostawiam dla zachowania sygnatury, choć w tej logice nie jest używane 
42:     SHIFT_OPEN, 
43:     SHIFT_CLOSE, 
44:     START_SHIFT_MAP, 
45:     END_SHIFT_MAP 
46: ): 
47:     print("[OBJECTIVE] balance daily Morning vs Afternoon staff") 
48:     penalties = [] 
49:     # Iterujemy po dniach, a nie po pracownikach 
50:     for d in days: 
51:          
52:         # Sumujemy wszystkie poranne zmiany (Otwarcie + przesunięcia od otwarcia) w danym dniu 
53:         morning_shifts = sum( 
54:             x[e, d, s] 
55:             for e in range(len(employees)) 
56:             for s in [SHIFT_OPEN] + list(START_SHIFT_MAP.keys()) 
57:         ) 
58:         # Sumujemy wszystkie popołudniowe zmiany (Zamknięcie + przesunięcia do zamknięcia) w danym dniu 
59:         afternoon_shifts = sum( 
60:             x[e, d, s] 
61:             for e in range(len(employees)) 
62:             for s in [SHIFT_CLOSE] + list(END_SHIFT_MAP.keys()) 
63:         ) 
64:         # Obliczamy różnicę między rano a popo 
65:         diff = model.NewIntVar(-50, 50, f"daily_balance_diff_d{d}") 
66:         model.Add(diff == morning_shifts - afternoon_shifts) 
67:         # Pobieramy wartość absolutną różnicy (aby karać za odchylenia w obie strony) 
68:         abs_diff = model.NewIntVar(0, 50, f"daily_balance_abs_d{d}") 
69:         model.AddAbsEquality(abs_diff, diff) 
70:         # Dodajemy różnicę do ogólnej puli kar (im większa różnica, tym gorzej) 
71:         penalties.append(abs_diff) 
72:     return penalties 
73: def add_edge_shift_bonus( 
74:     model, 
75:     x, 
76:     employees, 
77:     days, 
78:     SHIFT_WORK_START_15, 
79:     SHIFT_WORK_END_15 
80: ): 
81:     penalties = [] 
82:     for d in days: 
83:         start_15_exists = model.NewBoolVar(f"start15_exists_d{d}") 
84:         end_15_exists = model.NewBoolVar(f"end15_exists_d{d}") 
85:         # czy ktoś ma +15 od otwarcia 
86:         model.Add( 
87:             sum(x[e, d, SHIFT_WORK_START_15] for e in range(len(employees))) >= 1 
88:         ).OnlyEnforceIf(start_15_exists) 
89:         model.Add( 
90:             sum(x[e, d, SHIFT_WORK_START_15] for e in range(len(employees))) == 0 
91:         ).OnlyEnforceIf(start_15_exists.Not()) 
92:         # czy ktoś ma -15 od zamknięcia 
93:         model.Add( 
94:             sum(x[e, d, SHIFT_WORK_END_15] for e in range(len(employees))) >= 1 
95:         ).OnlyEnforceIf(end_15_exists) 
96:         model.Add( 
97:             sum(x[e, d, SHIFT_WORK_END_15] for e in range(len(employees))) == 0 
98:         ).OnlyEnforceIf(end_15_exists.Not()) 
99:         # kara jeśli brak 
100:         penalties.append(start_15_exists.Not()) 
101:         penalties.append(end_15_exists.Not()) 
102:     return penalties

17: ):

23:         eff_hours = get_effective_daily_hours(emp, shop) 
24:         for i in range(len(days) - 1): 
25:             d = days[i] 
26:             d_next = days[i + 1] 
27:             if d not in trade_days or d_next not in trade_days: 
28:                 continue

30:             hours_next = shop.get_open_hours_for_day(d_next)

34:             open_next, close_next = hours_next

39:             ) 
40:             shifts_today[SHIFT_CLOSE] = ( 
41:                 calc_start(close_d, eff_hours), 
42:                 close_d 
43:             ) 
44:             for shift, off in START_SHIFTS.items(): 
45:                 start = ( 
46:                     datetime.strptime(open_d, fmt) 
47:                     + timedelta(minutes=off) 
48:                 ).strftime(fmt) 
49:                 shifts_today[shift] = ( 
50:                     start, 
51:                     calc_end(start, eff_hours) 
52:                 ) 
53:             for shift, off in END_SHIFTS.items(): 
54:                 end = ( 
55:                     datetime.strptime(close_d, fmt) 
56:                     - timedelta(minutes=off) 
57:                 ).strftime(fmt) 
58:                 shifts_today[shift] = ( 
59:                     calc_start(end, eff_hours), 
60:                     end 
61:                 ) 
62:             shifts_next = {} 
63:             shifts_next[SHIFT_OPEN] = ( 
64:                 open_next, 
65:                 calc_end(open_next, eff_hours) 
66:             ) 
67:             shifts_next[SHIFT_CLOSE] = ( 
68:                 calc_start(close_next, eff_hours), 
69:                 close_next 
70:             ) 
71:             for shift, off in START_SHIFTS.items(): 
72:                 start = ( 
73:                     datetime.strptime(open_next, fmt) 
74:                     + timedelta(minutes=off) 
75:                 ).strftime(fmt) 
76:                 shifts_next[shift] = ( 
77:                     start, 
78:                     calc_end(start, eff_hours) 
79:                 )

84:                 ).strftime(fmt)

87:                     end 
88:                 ) 
89:             for s1 in shifts_today: 
90:                 for s2 in shifts_next: 
91:                     end_today = datetime.strptime(shifts_today[s1][1], fmt) 
92:                     start_next = datetime.strptime(shifts_next[s2][0], fmt) 
93:                     rest = start_next - end_today 
94:                     if rest.total_seconds() < 0: 
95:                         rest += timedelta(days=1) 
96:                     if rest < timedelta(hours=11): 
97:                         if not soft: 
98:                             model.Add( 
99:                                 x[e, d, s1] + x[e, d_next, s2] <= 1 
100:                             ) 
101:                         else: 
102:                             violation = model.NewBoolVar( 
103:                                 f"rest_violation_e{e}_d{d}_{s1}_{s2}" 
104:                             ) 
105:                             model.Add( 
106:                                 x[e, d, s1] + x[e, d_next, s2] <= 1 + violation 
107:                             ) 
108:                             violations.append(violation) 
109:                         rest_constraints += 1 
110:     print("Constrainty 11h rest:", rest_constraints) 
111:     return violations

31:         )

55:                 end = calc_end(open_time, eff_hours) 
56:                 schedule.set_day_hours(emp, d, open_time, end) 
57:                 continue

59:                 start = calc_start(close_time, eff_hours) 
60:                 schedule.set_day_hours(emp, d, start, close_time) 
61:                 continue 
62:             assigned = False 
63:             for shift, offset in START_SHIFTS.items(): 
64:                 if solver.Value(x[e, d, shift]) == 1: 
65:                     start_dt = datetime.strptime(open_time, fmt) + timedelta(minutes=offset) 
66:                     start = start_dt.strftime(fmt) 
67:                     end = calc_end(start, eff_hours) 
68:                     schedule.set_day_hours(emp, d, start, end) 
69:                     assigned = True 
70:                     break 
71:             if not assigned: 
72:                 for shift, offset in END_SHIFTS.items(): 
73:                     if solver.Value(x[e, d, shift]) == 1: 
74:                         end_dt = datetime.strptime(close_time, fmt) - timedelta(minutes=offset) 
75:                         end = end_dt.strftime(fmt) 
76:                         start = calc_start(end, eff_hours) 
77:                         schedule.set_day_hours(emp, d, start, end) 
78:                         break 
79:     print("=== KONIEC GENERATORA ===")

1: from ortools.sat.python import cp_model 
2: def build_objective(model, weighted_soft_terms): 
3:     if weighted_soft_terms: 
4:         print("[OBJECTIVE] terms:", len(weighted_soft_terms)) 
5:         model.Maximize(-sum(weighted_soft_terms)) 
6: def solve_model(model): 
7:     solver = cp_model.CpSolver() 
8:     solver.parameters.max_time_in_seconds = 60 
9:     solver.parameters.num_search_workers = 10 
10:     status = solver.Solve(model) 
11:     print("Status:", solver.StatusName(status)) 
12:     print("Czas:", solver.WallTime(), "s") 
13:     print("Conflicts:", solver.NumConflicts()) 
14:     print("Branches:", solver.NumBranches()) 
15:     return solver, status

1: def get_effective_daily_hours(emp, shop): 
2:     # 🔴 specjalny fulltime max 8h 
3:     if emp.employment_fraction == 1.01: 
4:         hours = 8.0 
5:     # 🔴 normalny fulltime z wymuszeniem 8:30 
6:     elif shop.constraints.get("force_fulltime_845", False) and emp.employment_fraction == 1.0: 
7:         hours = 8.50 
8:     else: 
9:         hours = 8.0 * emp.employment_fraction 
10:     minutes = int(hours * 60) 
11:     minutes = (minutes // 15) * 15 
12:     return minutes / 60

1: from dataclasses import dataclass 
2: from typing import List 
3: from model.month_schedule import MonthSchedule 
4: from model.shop_config import ShopConfig 
5: from model.constraint_policy import ConstraintPolicy 
6: # ========================================== 
7: # RESULT 
8: # ========================================== 
9: @dataclass 
10: class ConstraintViolation: 
11:     type: str          # np. "max_consecutive_days" 
12:     employee: object   # Employee lub None (jeśli globalne) 
13:     day: int | None 
14:     message: str 
15: # ========================================== 
16: # ENGINE 
17: # ========================================== 
18: # ========================================== 
19: # RULE BASE 
20: # ========================================== 
21: class Rule: 
22:     def apply(self, schedule: MonthSchedule, shop: ShopConfig) -> List[ConstraintViolation]: 
23:         raise NotImplementedError 
24: # ========================================== 
25: # RULES 
26: # ========================================== 
27: class MaxConsecutiveDaysRule(Rule): 
28:     def __init__(self, limit: int): 
29:         self.limit = limit 
30:     def apply(self, schedule, shop): 
31:         results = [] 
32:         for emp in schedule.employees: 
33:             streak = 0 
34:             for day in range(1, schedule.days_in_month + 1): 
35:                 ds = schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
36:                 if not ds.is_empty(): 
37:                     streak += 1 
38:                 else: 
39:                     streak = 0 
40:                 if streak > self.limit: 
41:                     results.append( 
42:                         ConstraintViolation( 
43:                             type="max_consecutive_days", 
44:                             employee=emp, 
45:                             day=day, 
46:                             message=f"Więcej niż {self.limit} dni pracy pod rząd" 
47:                         ) 
48:                     ) 
49:         return results 
50: class MinStaffRule(Rule): 
51:     def __init__(self, minimum: int, mode: str): 
52:         self.minimum = minimum 
53:         self.mode = mode  # "open" | "close" 
54:     def apply(self, schedule, shop): 
55:         results = [] 
56:         for day in range(1, schedule.days_in_month + 1): 
57:             stats = opening_closing_stats(schedule, shop, day, self.mode) 
58:             if stats["count"] < self.minimum: 
59:                 results.append( 
60:                     ConstraintViolation( 
61:                         type=f"min_{self.mode}_staff", 
62:                         employee=None, 
63:                         day=day, 
64:                         message=( 
65:                             f"Za mało osób na " 
66:                             f"{'otwarciu' if self.mode == 'open' else 'zamknięciu'} " 
67:                             f"(min {self.minimum})" 
68:                         ) 
69:                     ) 
70:                 ) 
71:         return results 
72: class Rest11hRule(Rule): 
73:     def apply(self, schedule, shop): 
74:         from datetime import datetime, timedelta 
75:         results = [] 
76:         fmt = "%H:%M" 
77:         for emp in schedule.employees: 
78:             for day in range(1, schedule.days_in_month): 
79:                 today = schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
80:                 next_day = schedule.get_day(emp, day + 1) 
81:                 if today.is_empty() or next_day.is_empty(): 
82:                     continue 
83:                 end_today = datetime.strptime(today.end, fmt) 
84:                 start_next = datetime.strptime(next_day.start, fmt) 
85:                 start_next += timedelta(days=1) 
86:                 rest = start_next - end_today 
87:                 hours = rest.total_seconds() / 3600 
88:                 if hours < 11: 
89:                     results.append( 
90:                         ConstraintViolation( 
91:                             type="rest_11h", 
92:                             employee=emp, 
93:                             day=day + 1, 
94:                             message=f"Przerwa tylko {round(hours,2)}h (min 11h)" 
95:                         ) 
96:                     ) 
97:         return results 
98: class MeatCoverageRule(Rule): 
99:     def apply(self, schedule, shop): 
100:         results = [] 
101:         for day in range(1, schedule.days_in_month + 1): 
102:             result = meat_coverage(schedule, shop, day) 
103:             if not result["ok"]: 
104:                 if result["gap"] is None: 
105:                     msg = "Brak mięsa przez cały dzień" 
106:                 else: 
107:                     start, end = result["gap"] 
108:                     msg = f"Brak mięsa od {start} do {end}" 
109:                 results.append( 
110:                     ConstraintViolation( 
111:                         type="meat_coverage", 
112:                         employee=None, 
113:                         day=day, 
114:                         message=msg 
115:                     ) 
116:                 ) 
117:         return results 
118: # ========================================== 
119: # ENGINE 
120: # ========================================== 
121: class ConstraintEngine: 
122:     @staticmethod 
123:     def evaluate(schedule: MonthSchedule, 
124:                  shop: ShopConfig) -> List[ConstraintViolation]: 
125:         cfg = shop.constraints 
126:         rules: List[Rule] = [] 
127:         if "max_consecutive_days" in cfg: 
128:             rules.append( 
129:                 MaxConsecutiveDaysRule(cfg["max_consecutive_days"]) 
130:             ) 
131:         if "min_open_staff" in cfg: 
132:             rules.append( 
133:                 MinStaffRule(cfg["min_open_staff"], "open") 
134:             ) 
135:         if "min_close_staff" in cfg: 
136:             rules.append( 
137:                 MinStaffRule(cfg["min_close_staff"], "close") 
138:             ) 
139:         if cfg.get("enforce_11h_rest", False): 
140:             rules.append(Rest11hRule()) 
141:         meat_policy = shop.constraint_policies.get("meat_coverage", ConstraintPolicy.MANDATORY) 
142:         if meat_policy  
143:             rules.append(MeatCoverageRule()) 
144:         violations: List[ConstraintViolation] = [] 
145:         for rule in rules: 
146:             violations.extend(rule.apply(schedule, shop)) 
147:         return violations 
148: def opening_closing_stats( 
149:     schedule: MonthSchedule, 
150:     shop: ShopConfig, 
151:     day: int, 
152:     mode: str  # "open" | "close" 
153: ) -> dict: 
154:     open_hours = shop.get_open_hours_for_day(day) 
155:     if not open_hours: 
156:         return {"count": 0, "has_opener": False} 
157:     target_time = open_hours[0] if mode == "open" else open_hours[1] 
158:     count = 0 
159:     has_opener = False 
160:     for emp in schedule.employees: 
161:         ds = schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
162:         if ds.is_empty(): 
163:             continue 
164:         time = ds.start if mode == "open" else ds.end 
165:         if time == target_time: 
166:             count += 1 
167:             if emp.is_opener: 
168:                 has_opener = True 
169:     return {"count": count, "has_opener": has_opener} 
170: # ================================================== 
171: # MIĘSO 
172: # ================================================== 
173: from datetime import datetime 
174: FMT = "%H:%M" 
175: def meat_coverage(schedule, shop, day: int) -> dict: 
176:     """ 
177:     Sprawdza czy mięso jest pokryte przez cały dzień. 
178:     Wersja zoptymalizowana – analiza przedziałowa. 
179:     """ 
180:     open_hours = shop.get_open_hours_for_day(day) 
181:     if not open_hours: 
182:         return {"ok": True, "gap": None} 
183:     open_t = datetime.strptime(open_hours[0], FMT) 
184:     close_t = datetime.strptime(open_hours[1], FMT) 
185:     intervals = [] 
186:     # 🔹 zbierz przedziały tylko dla pracowników z mięsem 
187:     for emp in schedule.employees: 
188:         if not emp.is_meat: 
189:             continue 
190:         ds = schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
191:         if ds.is_empty(): 
192:             continue 
193:         start = datetime.strptime(ds.start, FMT) 
194:         end = datetime.strptime(ds.end, FMT) 
195:         # ignorujemy błędne zakresy 
196:         if start >= end: 
197:             continue 
198:         intervals.append((start, end)) 
199:     if not intervals: 
200:         return { 
201:             "ok": False, 
202:             "gap": (open_t.strftime(FMT), close_t.strftime(FMT)) 
203:         } 
204:     # 🔹 sortujemy po czasie rozpoczęcia 
205:     intervals.sort(key=lambda x: x[0]) 
206:     current = open_t 
207:     for start, end in intervals: 
208:         # luka przed kolejnym przedziałem 
209:         if start > current: 
210:             return { 
211:                 "ok": False, 
212:                 "gap": (current.strftime(FMT), start.strftime(FMT)) 
213:             } 
214:         # rozszerzamy pokrycie 
215:         if end > current: 
216:             current = end 
217:         if current >= close_t: 
218:             return {"ok": True, "gap": None} 
219:     # jeśli po przejściu wszystkich nadal nie pokryliśmy 
220:     if current < close_t: 
221:         return { 
222:             "ok": False, 
223:             "gap": (current.strftime(FMT), close_t.strftime(FMT)) 
224:         } 
225:     return {"ok": True, "gap": None} 
226: def rest_11h_violation(schedule: MonthSchedule, emp, day: int): 
227:     """ 
228:     Zwraca: 
229:     { 
230:         "violation": bool, 
231:         "rest_hours": float | None 
232:     } 
233:     """ 
234:     if day < 1 or day >= schedule.days_in_month: 
235:         return {"violation": False, "rest_hours": None} 
236:     today = schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
237:     next_day = schedule.get_day(emp, day + 1) 
238:     if today.is_empty() or next_day.is_empty(): 
239:         return {"violation": False, "rest_hours": None} 
240:     from datetime import datetime, timedelta 
241:     fmt = "%H:%M" 
242:     end_today = datetime.strptime(today.end, fmt) 
243:     start_next = datetime.strptime(next_day.start, fmt) 
244:     # 🔥 uwzględnij przejście do kolejnego dnia 
245:     start_next += timedelta(days=1) 
246:     rest = start_next - end_today 
247:     hours = rest.total_seconds() / 3600 
248:     return { 
249:         "violation": hours < 11, 
250:         "rest_hours": round(hours, 2) 
251:     }

4:     PREFERRED = "PREFERRED"     # soft – jeśli możliwe 
5:     DISABLED = "DISABLED"       # wyłączony

FILE: C:\Users\kewi1\Desktop\Grafik dino V2\model\day_schedule.py 
================================================== 
 
1: from dataclasses import dataclass 
2: from datetime import datetime, timedelta 
3: _TIME_FORMAT = "%H:%M" 
4: def _parse_time(value: str) -> datetime: 
5:     return datetime.strptime(value, _TIME_FORMAT) 
6: @dataclass 
7: class DaySchedule: 
8:     """ 
9:     Jedna komórka grafiku (jeden dzień, jeden pracownik). 
10:     Przechowuje: 
11:     - start pracy 
12:     - koniec pracy 
13:     - flagę urlopu 
14:     """ 
15:     start: str | None = None 
16:     end: str | None = None 
17:     is_leave: bool = False   # ← DODANE 
18:     is_locked: bool = False 
19:     is_sick: bool = False 
20:     def is_empty(self) -> bool: 
21:         """Czy dzień jest pusty (wolne).""" 
22:         return self.start is None and self.end is None 
23:     def set_free(self) -> None: 
24:         """Ustawia dzień jako wolny.""" 
25:         self.start = None 
26:         self.end = None 
27:         self.is_leave = False 
28:         self.is_sick = False 
29:     def set_leave(self) -> None: 
30:         """Ustawia dzień jako urlop.""" 
31:         self.start = None 
32:         self.end = None 
33:         self.is_leave = True 
34:         self.is_sick = False   
35:     def set_hours(self, start: str, end: str) -> None: 
36:         """ 
37:         Ustawia godziny pracy. 
38:         Format: 'HH:MM' 
39:         """ 
40:         start_dt = _parse_time(start) 
41:         end_dt = _parse_time(end) 
42:         if end_dt <= start_dt: 
43:             raise ValueError("Godzina zakończenia musi być późniejsza niż rozpoczęcia") 
44:         self.start = start 
45:         self.end = end 
46:         self.is_leave = False 
47:         self.is_sick = False 
48:     def total_duration(self) -> timedelta | None: 
49:         """ 
50:         Zwraca czas pracy jako timedelta. 
51:         """ 
52:         if self.is_empty() or self.is_leave or self.is_sick: 
53:             return None 
54:         start_dt = _parse_time(self.start) 
55:         end_dt = _parse_time(self.end) 
56:         return end_dt - start_dt 
57:     def total_as_str(self) -> str | None: 
58:         """ 
59:         Zwraca sumę godzin w formacie HH:MM. 
60:         """ 
61:         duration = self.total_duration() 
62:         if duration is None: 
63:             return None 
64:         total_minutes = int(duration.total_seconds() // 60) 
65:         hours = total_minutes // 60 
66:         minutes = total_minutes % 60 
67:         return f"{hours}:{minutes:02d}" 
68:     def as_rows(self) -> tuple[str, str, str]: 
69:         """ 
70:         Zwraca dane dokładnie pod GUI: 
71:         (start, end, suma) 
72:         Jeśli wolne → puste stringi 
73:         Jeśli urlop → 🌴 w pierwszej linii 
74:         """ 
75:         if self.is_leave: 
76:             return "🌴", "", "" 
77:         if self.is_sick: 
78:             return "🤒", "", "" 
79:         if self.is_empty(): 
80:             return "", "", "" 
81:         return ( 
82:             self.start or "", 
83:             self.end or "", 
84:             self.total_as_str() or "", 
85:         ) 
86:     def set_sick(self): 
87:         self.start = None 
88:         self.end = None 
89:         self.is_leave = False 
90:         self.is_sick = True 
91:     def total_minutes(self, employee=None, shop=None) -> int: 
92:         # urlop / chorobowe 
93:         if self.is_leave or self.is_sick: 
94:             if employee and shop: 
95:                 from logic.generator.rest_constraint import get_effective_daily_hours 
96:                 return int(get_effective_daily_hours(employee, shop) * 60) 
97:             return 0 
98:         duration = self.total_duration() 
99:         if duration is None: 
100:             return 0 
101:         return int(duration.total_seconds() // 60) 
102: def calc_end(start_str: str, hours: float) -> str: 
103:     fmt = "%H:%M" 
104:     start = datetime.strptime(start_str, fmt) 
105:     end = start + timedelta(hours=hours) 
106:     return end.strftime(fmt) 
107: def calc_start(end_str: str, hours: float) -> str: 
108:     fmt = "%H:%M" 
109:     end = datetime.strptime(end_str, fmt) 
110:     start = end - timedelta(hours=hours) 
111:     return start.strftime(fmt)
