17:     is_meat: bool = field(default=False, compare=False) 
18:     no_night: bool = field(default=False, compare=False) 
19:     monthly_target_hours: int = field(default=160, compare=False) 
20:     daily_hours: int = field(default=8, compare=False)

27:     def validate(self) -> None: 
28:         if not self.last_name.strip(): 
29:             raise ValueError("Nazwisko nie może być puste") 
30:         if not self.first_name.strip(): 
31:             raise ValueError("Imię nie może być puste") 
32:         # 🔧 ZMIANA – luzujemy ograniczenie 
33:         if self.daily_hours <= 0: 
34:             raise ValueError("Dzienna liczba godzin musi być większa od 0") 
35:         # 🔥 NOWA WALIDACJA

37:             raise ValueError("Wymiar etatu musi być w zakresie (0, 1]")

40:                 raise ValueError("Nieprawidłowy dzień tygodnia") 
41:             if "start" not in cfg or "end" not in cfg:

22:         self.employees.sort() 
23:         self._data[employee] = { 
24:             day: DaySchedule() 
25:             for day in range(1, self.days_in_month + 1) 
26:         }

28:         if employee not in self._data: 
29:             return

1: import calendar 
2: from model.constraint_policy import ConstraintPolicy 
3: class ShopConfig: 
4:     """ 
5:     Konfiguracja sklepu: 
6:     - niedziele handlowe 
7:     - godziny otwarcia per dzień tygodnia 
8:     - constrainty 
9:     - opcje UI 
10:     """ 
11:     def __init__(self, year: int, month: int): 
12:         self.year = year 
13:         self.month = month 
14:         # Toggle dla constraintów z model.constraint_policy 
15:         self.constraint_policies = { 
16:             "rest_11h": ConstraintPolicy.MANDATORY, 
17:             "meat": ConstraintPolicy.PREFERRED, 
18:             "balance": ConstraintPolicy.PREFERRED, 
19:             "max_consecutive": ConstraintPolicy.PREFERRED, 
20:             "open": ConstraintPolicy.MANDATORY, 
21:             "close": ConstraintPolicy.MANDATORY, 
22:             "monthly_hours": ConstraintPolicy.PREFERRED, 
23:             "meat_coverage": ConstraintPolicy.PREFERRED, 
24:             "availability": ConstraintPolicy.MANDATORY, 
25:         } 
26:         # ----------------------------- 
27:         # Override godzin dla konkretnego dnia 
28:         # ----------------------------- 
29:         self.day_overrides: dict[int, tuple[str, str]] = {} 
30:         # ----------------------------- 
31:         # CONSTRAINTS CONFIG 
32:         # ----------------------------- 
33:         self.constraints = { 
34:             "max_consecutive_days": 4, 
35:             "min_open_staff": 3, 
36:             "min_close_staff": 3, 
37:             "force_fulltime_845": True, 
38:         } 
39:         # ----------------------------- 
40:         # UI OPTIONS 
41:         # ----------------------------- 
42:         # "compact" | "detailed" 
43:         self.cell_display_mode = "compact" 
44:         # ----------------------------- 
45:         # Niedziele handlowe 
46:         # ----------------------------- 
47:         self.trade_sundays: set[int] = set() 
48:         # dni ustawowo wolne 
49:         self.public_holidays: set[int] = set() 
50:         self.standard_daily_hours = 8.0 
51:         # ----------------------------- 
52:         # Godziny otwarcia per weekday 
53:         # (0=Pn ... 6=Nd) 
54:         # ----------------------------- 
55:         self.open_hours: dict[int, tuple[str, str]] = { 
56:             0: ("05:30", "23:00"), 
57:             1: ("05:30", "22:45"), 
58:             2: ("05:30", "22:45"), 
59:             3: ("05:30", "22:45"), 
60:             4: ("05:30", "22:45"), 
61:             5: ("05:30", "22:45"), 
62:             6: ("05:30", "22:45"),  # Nd (jeśli handlowa) 
63:         } 
64:     # ========================================================== 
65:     # PODSTAWOWE METODY 
66:     # ========================================================== 
67:     def weekday(self, day: int) -> int: 
68:         return calendar.weekday(self.year, self.month, day) 
69:     def is_sunday(self, day: int) -> bool: 
70:         return self.weekday(day) == 6 
71:     def is_trade_day(self, day: int) -> bool: 
72:         if day in self.public_holidays: 
73:             return False 
74:         if self.is_sunday(day): 
75:             return day in self.trade_sundays 
76:         return True 
77:     # ========================================================== 
78:     # GODZINY OTWARCIA 
79:     # ========================================================== 
80:     def get_open_hours_for_day(self, day: int) -> tuple[str, str] | None: 
81:         """ 
82:         Zwraca (open, close) albo None jeśli sklep zamknięty 
83:         lub brak poprawnych godzin. 
84:         """ 
85:         if not self.is_trade_day(day): 
86:             return None 
87:         # override ma najwyższy priorytet 
88:         if day in self.day_overrides: 
89:             start, end = self.day_overrides[day] 
90:             if start and end: 
91:                 return start, end 
92:             return None 
93:         wd = self.weekday(day) 
94:         hours = self.open_hours.get(wd) 
95:         if not hours: 
96:             return None 
97:         start, end = hours 
98:         if not start or not end: 
99:             return None 
100:         return start, end 
101:     def get_open_hours_for_weekday(self, weekday: int) -> tuple[str, str]: 
102:         return self.open_hours[weekday] 
103:     def set_open_hours_for_weekday(self, weekday: int, start: str, end: str): 
104:         self.open_hours[weekday] = (start, end) 
105:     # ========================================================== 
106:     # SERIALIZACJA 
107:     # ========================================================== 
108:     def to_dict(self): 
109:         return { 
110:             "year": self.year, 
111:             "month": self.month, 
112:             "open_hours": self.open_hours, 
113:             "trade_sundays": list(self.trade_sundays), 
114:             "day_overrides": self.day_overrides, 
115:             "constraints": self.constraints, 
116:             "cell_display_mode": self.cell_display_mode, 
117:             "public_holidays": list(self.public_holidays), 
118:             "standard_daily_hours": self.standard_daily_hours, 
119:         } 
120:     @classmethod 
121:     def from_dict(cls, data): 
122:         cfg = cls(data["year"], data["month"]) 
123:         # open_hours 
124:         cfg.open_hours = { 
125:             int(k): tuple(v) 
126:             for k, v in data.get("open_hours", {}).items() 
127:         } 
128:         # niedziele handlowe 
129:         cfg.trade_sundays = set(data.get("trade_sundays", [])) 
130:         #święta ustawowo wolne 
131:         cfg.public_holidays = set(data.get("public_holidays", [])) 
132:         # override dni 
133:         cfg.day_overrides = { 
134:             int(day): tuple(hours) 
135:             for day, hours in data.get("day_overrides", {}).items() 
136:         } 
137:         # constraints 
138:         saved_constraints = data.get("constraints", {}) 
139:         cfg.constraints.update(saved_constraints) 
140:         # UI 
141:         cfg.cell_display_mode = data.get("cell_display_mode", "compact") 
142:         cfg.standard_daily_hours = data.get("standard_daily_hours", 8.0) 
143:         return cfg 
144:     def get_full_time_nominal_hours(self) -> int: 
145:         """ 
146:         Zwraca nominalny wymiar czasu pracy (pełny etat) 
147:         dla danego miesiąca zgodnie z kodeksem pracy. 
148:         """ 
149:         import calendar 
150:         from datetime import date 
151:         workdays = 0 
152:         days_in_month = calendar.monthrange(self.year, self.month)[1] 
153:         for d in range(1, days_in_month + 1): 
154:             wd = calendar.weekday(self.year, self.month, d) 
155:             # pon–pt 
156:             if wd < 5: 
157:                 # jeśli to święto ustawowe → nie liczymy 
158:                 if d in self.public_holidays: 
159:                     continue 
160:                 workdays += 1 
161:         return workdays * 8

1: import calendar

4: try: 
5:     from ortools.sat.python import cp_model 
6:     from logic.auto_generator import AutoScheduleGenerator

11:     HAS_ORTOOLS = True

13:     HAS_ORTOOLS = False 
14: @unittest.skipUnless(HAS_ORTOOLS, "ortools is required for solver integration tests") 
15: class TestAutoScheduleGeneratorSolverEdgeCases(unittest.TestCase): 
16:     ACTIVE_DAYS = [3, 4, 5, 6, 7]  # celowo krótki horyzont dla szybszych testów 
17:     def _make_shop(self, year, month, rng): 
18:         shop = ShopConfig(year, month) 
19:         # Zostawiamy tylko kilka dni handlowych, resztę oznaczamy jako święta. 
20:         days_in_month = calendar.monthrange(year, month)[1] 
21:         shop.public_holidays = set(range(1, days_in_month + 1)) - set(self.ACTIVE_DAYS) 
22:         # Losowe godziny otwarcia (różne przypadki sklepów) 
23:         start = rng.choice(["05:30", "06:00", "06:30"]) 
24:         end = rng.choice(["20:00", "21:00", "22:00", "22:45"]) 
25:         for wd in range(7): 
26:             shop.open_hours[wd] = (start, end) 
27:         # Upraszczamy pozostałe constrainty, żeby testować głównie obsadę i role. 
28:         for key in ["rest_11h", "balance", "max_consecutive", "monthly_hours", "meat_coverage"]: 
29:             shop.constraint_policies[key] = ConstraintPolicy.DISABLED 
30:         shop.constraint_policies["open"] = ConstraintPolicy.MANDATORY 
31:         shop.constraint_policies["close"] = ConstraintPolicy.MANDATORY 
32:         shop.constraint_policies["meat"] = ConstraintPolicy.MANDATORY 
33:         return shop 
34:     def _build_case(self, rng, force_infeasible=False): 
35:         year, month = 2025, 2 
36:         schedule = MonthSchedule(year, month) 
37:         shop = self._make_shop(year, month, rng) 
38:         num_employees = rng.randint(5, 8) 
39:         if force_infeasible: 
40:             # Edge-case: za mało openerów i/lub mięsa + za wysoka wymagana obsada 
41:             opener_count = rng.randint(0, 1) 
42:             meat_count = rng.randint(0, 1) 
43:             min_open = rng.randint(3, 5) 
44:             min_close = rng.randint(3, 5) 
45:         else: 
46:             # Feasible: minimum 2 openerów i 2 mięsa (open + close każdego dnia) 
47:             opener_count = rng.randint(2, min(4, num_employees)) 
48:             meat_count = rng.randint(2, min(4, num_employees)) 
49:             min_open = rng.randint(2, 3) 
50:             min_close = rng.randint(2, 3) 
51:         opener_indices = set(rng.sample(range(num_employees), k=opener_count)) 
52:         meat_indices = set(rng.sample(range(num_employees), k=meat_count)) 
53:         for i in range(num_employees): 
54:             emp = Employee(

77:         if end: 
78:             self._manual_end = True 
79:             self.end_edit.set_time_str(end) 
80:     def _suggest_end(self): 
81:         if self._manual_end: 
82:             self._update_duration() 
83:             return 
84:         start_qt = _parse_time(self.start_edit.get_time_str()) 
85:         suggested = start_qt.addSecs(self.daily_hours * 3600) 
86:          
87:         if suggested > self._open_end_qt: 
88:             suggested = self._open_end_qt 
89:         self._updating = True 
90:         self.end_edit.set_time_str(suggested.toString("HH:mm")) 
91:         self._updating = False 
92:         self._update_duration() 
93:     def _on_start_changed(self, _): 
94:         if self._updating: 
95:             return 
96:         if not self._manual_end: 
97:             self._suggest_end() 
98:         else: 
99:             self._update_duration() 
100:     def _on_end_changed(self, _): 
101:         if self._updating: 
102:             return 
103:         self._manual_end = True 
104:         self._update_duration() 
105:     def _update_duration(self): 
106:         start_qt = _parse_time(self.start_edit.get_time_str()) 
107:         end_qt = _parse_time(self.end_edit.get_time_str()) 
108:          
109:         minutes = start_qt.secsTo(end_qt) // 60 
110:         if minutes < 0: 
111:             minutes = 0 
112:         hours = minutes // 60 
113:         mins = minutes % 60 
114:         self.duration_label.setText(f"Czas pracy: {hours}:{mins:02d}") 
115:     def _set_free(self): 
116:         self.result_mode = "free" 
117:         self.accept() 
118:     def _set_leave(self): 
119:         self.result_mode = "leave" 
120:         self.accept() 
121:     def _set_sick(self): 
122:         self.result_mode = "sick" 
123:         self.accept() 
124:     def _save(self): 
125:         start_str = self.start_edit.get_time_str() 
126:         end_str = self.end_edit.get_time_str() 
127:          
128:         start_qt = _parse_time(start_str) 
129:         end_qt = _parse_time(end_str) 
130:         if end_qt <= start_qt: 
131:             QMessageBox.critical(self, "Błąd", "Koniec musi być później niż start.") 
132:             return 
133:         if start_qt < self._open_start_qt or end_qt > self._open_end_qt: 
134:             QMessageBox.critical(self, "Błąd", "Godziny muszą mieścić się w czasie otwarcia sklepu.") 
135:             return 
136:         self.result_mode = "hours" 
137:         self.result_start = start_str 
138:         self.result_end = end_str 
139:         self.accept()

67:             QMessageBox.critical(self, "Błąd", "Godziny nie mogą być puste.") 
68:             return 
69:         start_qt = _parse_time(start_str) 
70:         end_qt = _parse_time(end_str) 
71:         if end_qt <= start_qt: 
72:             QMessageBox.critical(self, "Błąd", "Zamknięcie musi być później niż otwarcie.") 
73:             return 
74:         self.result_mode = "save" 
75:         self.result_start = start_str 
76:         self.result_end = end_str 
77:         self.result_holiday = self.holiday_box.isChecked() 
78:         self.accept()

FILE: C:\Users\kewi1\Desktop\Grafik dino V2\ui\demo_manager.py

1: from PySide6.QtWidgets import QMessageBox 
2: import os 
3: import json 
4: DEMO_FILE = "demo.json" 
5: class DemoManager: 
6:     MAX_GENERATIONS = 5 
7:     def __init__(self): 
8:         self.is_demo = True 
9:         self.generations_used = 0 
10:         self._load() 
11:     def can_generate(self, parent): 
12:         if not self.is_demo: 
13:             return True 
14:         if self.generations_used >= self.MAX_GENERATIONS: 
15:             QMessageBox.warning( 
16:                 parent, 
17:                 "Limit wersji demo", 
18:                 "Wykorzystałeś limit generowania grafiku.\n\nAby kontynuować, aktywuj pełną wersję." 
19:             ) 
20:             return False 
21:         return True 
22:     def register_generation(self): 
23:         if not self.is_demo: 
24:             return 
25:         self.generations_used += 1 
26:         self._save() 
27:     def get_remaining_generations(self): 
28:         if not self.is_demo: 
29:             return None 
30:         return self.MAX_GENERATIONS - self.generations_used 
31:     def block_save(self, parent): 
32:         if not self.is_demo: 
33:             return False 
34:         QMessageBox.warning( 
35:             parent, 
36:             "Wersja demo", 
37:             "Zapis projektu dostępny tylko w pełnej wersji." 
38:         ) 
39:         return True 
40:     def block_export(self, parent): 
41:         if not self.is_demo: 
42:             return False 
43:         QMessageBox.warning( 
44:             parent, 
45:             "Wersja demo", 
46:             "Eksport dostępny tylko w pełnej wersji." 
47:         ) 
48:         return True 
49:     def show_after_generate(self, parent): 
50:         if not self.is_demo: 
51:             return 
52:         QMessageBox.information( 
53:             parent, 
54:             "Wersja demo", 
55:             "Grafik wygenerowany.\n\nAby zapisać lub wyeksportować – wymagana pełna wersja." 
56:         ) 
57:     def _save(self): 
58:         try: 
59:             with open(DEMO_FILE, "w") as f: 
60:                 json.dump({"generations_used": self.generations_used}, f) 
61:         except: 
62:             pass 
63:     def _load(self): 
64:         if not os.path.exists(DEMO_FILE): 
65:             return 
66:         try: 
67:             with open(DEMO_FILE, "r") as f: 
68:                 data = json.load(f) 
69:                 self.generations_used = data.get("generations_used", 0) 
70:         except: 
71:             pass

154:         if idx >= 0:

156:     def _save(self): 
157:         ln = self.last_name.text().strip() 
158:         fn = self.first_name.text().strip() 
159:         if not ln or not fn: 
160:             QMessageBox.critical(self, "Błąd", "Imię i nazwisko nie mogą być puste.") 
161:             return 
162:         try: 
163:             emp = Employee( 
164:                 last_name=ln, 
165:                 first_name=fn, 
166:                 is_opener=self.is_opener.isChecked(), 
167:                 is_meat=self.is_meat.isChecked(), 
168:                 no_night=self.no_night.isChecked(), 
169:                 monthly_target_hours=self.monthly_target_hours.value(), 
170:                 employment_fraction=self.employment_fraction.currentData(), 
171:             ) 
172:             emp.validate() 
173:         except Exception as exc: 
174:             QMessageBox.critical(self, "Błąd", str(exc)) 
175:             return 
176:         self.employee_result = emp 
177:         self.accept() 
178:     def _delete_employee(self): 
179:         reply = QMessageBox.question( 
180:             self, 
181:             "Usuń pracownika", 
182:             f"Czy na pewno chcesz usunąć pracownika: {self.employee.first_name} {self.employee.last_name}?", 
183:             QMessageBox.Yes | QMessageBox.No, 
184:         ) 
185:         if reply == QMessageBox.Yes: 
186:             self.employee_result = None 
187:             self.accept()

43:         if item: 
44:             row = item.row() 
45:             if row  
46:                 self.hovered_row = row

48:         super().mouseMoveEvent(event) 
49:     def leaveEvent(self, event): 
50:         self.hovered_row = None 
51:         self.viewport().update() 
52:         super().leaveEvent(event) 
53:     def set_data( 
54:         self, 
55:         schedule, 
56:         shop_config, 
57:         controller, 
58:         main_window=None, 
59:         on_edit_day=None, 
60:         on_edit_employee=None, 
61:         on_context_menu=None, 
62:         on_header_menu=None, 
63:     ): 
64:         self.schedule = schedule 
65:         self.shop_config = shop_config 
66:         self.controller = controller 
67:         self.main_window = main_window 
68:         self.on_edit_day = on_edit_day 
69:         self.on_edit_employee = on_edit_employee 
70:         self.on_context_menu = on_context_menu 
71:         self.on_header_menu = on_header_menu 
72:     def build(self): 
73:         self.clear() 
74:         self.setSortingEnabled(False) 
75:         if not self.schedule or not self.shop_config: 
76:             self.setRowCount(0) 
77:             self.setColumnCount(0) 
78:             return 
79:         days = self.schedule.days_in_month 
80:         weekday_names = ["Pn", "Wt", "Śr", "Cz", "Pt", "So", "Nd"] 
81:         headers = ["Pracownik"] 
82:         for day in range(1, days + 1): 
83:             wd = calendar.weekday(self.schedule.year, self.schedule.month, day) 
84:             headers.append(f"{weekday_names[wd]}\n{day}") 
85:         headers.extend(["Praca\n(h)", "Urlop\n(h)", "L4\n(h)", "Razem\n(h)"]) 
86:         self.setColumnCount(len(headers)) 
87:         self.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(headers) 
88:         self.setRowCount(len(self.schedule.employees) + 4) 
89:         self.setColumnWidth(0, 180) 
90:         for col in range(1, days + 1): 
91:             if self.compact_mode: 
92:                 self.setColumnWidth(col, 30) 
93:             else: 
94:                 self.setColumnWidth(col, 60) 
95:         for col in range(days + 1, days + 5): 
96:             self.setColumnWidth(col, 60) 
97:         self.verticalHeader().setVisible(False) 
98:         self.horizontalHeader().setDefaultAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter) 
99:         self.horizontalHeader().setStretchLastSection(False) 
100:         for row in range(self.rowCount()): 
101:             self.setRowHeight(row, 42) 
102:     def refresh(self): 
103:         if not self.schedule or not self.shop_config: 
104:             return 
105:         self.build() 
106:         presenter = SchedulePresenter(self.schedule, self.shop_config) 
107:         constraint_presenter = ConstraintPresenter(self.schedule, self.shop_config) 
108:         # 3. Flaga sprawdzania limitu dni pod rząd z konfiguracji (domyślnie False) 
109:         hl_consecutive = self.shop_config.constraints.get("highlight_max_consecutive", False) 
110:         days = self.schedule.days_in_month 
111:         emp_count = len(self.schedule.employees) 
112:         for row, emp in enumerate(self.schedule.employees): 
113:             self._fill_employee_name(row, emp) 
114:             self._fill_day_cells(row, emp, days, presenter, constraint_presenter, hl_consecutive) 
115:             self._fill_summary_cells(row, emp, days) 
116:         self._fill_validation_rows(emp_count, days, constraint_presenter) 
117:         self.viewport().update() 
118:     def _fill_employee_name(self, row, emp): 
119:         item = QTableWidgetItem(emp.display_name()) 
120:         item.setData(Qt.UserRole, emp) 
121:         item.setTextAlignment(Qt.AlignVCenter | Qt.AlignLeft) 
122:         if emp.is_opener and emp.is_meat: 
123:             size = 32 
124:             pixmap = QPixmap(size * 2, size) 
125:             pixmap.fill(Qt.transparent) 
126:             painter = QPainter(pixmap) 
127:             painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing) 
128:             painter.drawPixmap(0, 0, self.icon_open.pixmap(size, size)) 
129:             painter.drawPixmap(size, 0, self.icon_meat.pixmap(size, size)) 
130:             painter.end() 
131:             item.setIcon(QIcon(pixmap)) 
132:         elif emp.is_opener: 
133:             item.setIcon(self.icon_open) 
134:         elif emp.is_meat: 
135:             item.setIcon(self.icon_meat) 
136:         font = QFont() 
137:         font.setBold(True) 
138:         item.setFont(font) 
139:         item.setBackground(QBrush(QColor(theme.BG_PANEL))) 
140:         self.setItem(row, 0, item) 
141:     def _fill_day_cells(self, row, emp, days, presenter, constraint_presenter, hl_consecutive): 
142:         for day in range(1, days + 1): 
143:             ds = self.schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
144:             item = QTableWidgetItem() 
145:             item.setTextAlignment(Qt.AlignHCenter | Qt.AlignVCenter) 
146:             item.setData(Qt.UserRole, (emp, day)) 
147:             if getattr(ds, "is_locked", False) and not ds.start and not ds.end and not ds.is_leave and not getattr(ds, "is_sick", False): 
148:                 item.setText("")  # Czyścimy tekst, żeby nie śmiecił 
149:                  
150:                 # Tworzymy profesjonalne szrafowanie (ukośne linie) 
151:                 brush = QBrush(QColor(205, 205, 205)) # Bardzo jasny szary 
152:                 brush.setStyle(Qt.BDiagPattern)       # Wzór: ukośne linie (Back Diagonal) 
153:                  
154:                 item.setBackground(brush) 
155:                 item.setToolTip("Dzień wolny (Zablokowany: Generator nie zmieni tego ustawienia)") 
156:                 self.setItem(row, day, item) 
157:                 continue 
158:             if hasattr(ds, "is_sick") and ds.is_sick: 
159:                 item.setText("🤒") 
160:                 item.setBackground(QBrush(QColor("#FFA07A"))) 
161:                 item.setToolTip("Chorobowe") 
162:                 self.setItem(row, day, item) 
163:                 continue 
164:             if ds.is_leave: 
165:                 item.setText("🌴") 
166:                 item.setBackground(QBrush(QColor(theme.OK_GREEN))) 
167:                 item.setToolTip("Urlop") 
168:                 self.setItem(row, day, item) 
169:                 continue 
170:             if not self.shop_config.is_trade_day(day): 
171:                 item.setBackground(QBrush(QColor(theme.BG_DISABLED))) 
172:                 self.setItem(row, day, item) 
173:                 continue 
174:             cell_view = presenter.get_cell_view(emp, day) 
175:             if self.compact_mode: 
176:                 text = "" 
177:                 hours = self.shop_config.get_open_hours_for_day(day) 
178:                 if hours and ds.start: 
179:                     open_start, open_end = hours 
180:                     from datetime import datetime 
181:                     fmt = "%H:%M" 
182:                     start = datetime.strptime(ds.start, fmt) 
183:                     shop_start = datetime.strptime(open_start, fmt) 
184:                     diff = (start - shop_start).total_seconds() / 60 
185:                     text = "2" if diff >= 120 else "1" 
186:                 item.setText(text) 
187:             else: 
188:                 lines = [line for line in [cell_view.text_start, cell_view.text_end, cell_view.text_total] if line] 
189:                 item.setText("\n".join(lines)) 
190:             item.setBackground(QBrush(QColor(cell_view.bg))) 
191:             if cell_view.tooltip: 
192:                 item.setToolTip(cell_view.tooltip) 
193:             # 3. Sprawdzanie błędów z flagą highlight_max_consecutive oraz zabezpieczenie przed błędem typu 
194:             error = constraint_presenter.get_cell_error(emp, day) 
195:             if error: 
196:                 has_consecutive = any(v.type == "max_consecutive_days" for v in error) 
197:                 if not has_consecutive or hl_consecutive: 
198:                     item.setBackground(QBrush(QColor(theme.ERR_RED))) 
199:                     item.setToolTip("\n".join(v.message for v in error if v.message)) 
200:             self.setItem(row, day, item) 
201:     def _fill_summary_cells(self, row, emp, days): 
202:         items = [ 
203:             QTableWidgetItem(self.schedule.total_hours_for_employee(emp)), 
204:             QTableWidgetItem(self.schedule.leave_hours_for_employee(emp)), 
205:             QTableWidgetItem(self.schedule.sick_hours_for_employee(emp)), 
206:             QTableWidgetItem(self.schedule.total_with_leave_and_sick_for_employee(emp)), 
207:         ] 
208:         for idx, item in enumerate(items, start=days + 1): 
209:             item.setTextAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter) 
210:             item.setBackground(QBrush(QColor(theme.BG_PANEL))) 
211:             self.setItem(row, idx, item) 
212:     def _fill_validation_rows(self, emp_count, days, constraint_presenter): 
213:         # Definiujemy wiersze podsumowania 
214:         rows = [ 
215:             ("Otwarcie", "open"),  
216:             ("Zamknięcie", "close"),  
217:             ("Rano/Popo", "morning_afternoon"),  
218:             ("Mięso", "meat") 
219:         ] 
220:         for offset, (label, key) in enumerate(rows): 
221:             row = emp_count + offset 
222:             # Etykieta wiersza (lewa kolumna) 
223:             name_item = QTableWidgetItem(label) 
224:             name_item.setBackground(QBrush(QColor(theme.BG_HEADER))) 
225:             name_item.setTextAlignment(Qt.AlignVCenter | Qt.AlignLeft) 
226:             self.setItem(row, 0, name_item) 
227:             for day in range(1, days + 1): 
228:                 # Liczniki dla danego dnia 
229:                 at_opening = 0 
230:                 at_closing = 0 
231:                 total_morning = 0 
232:                 total_afternoon = 0 
233:                 count_meat = 0 
234:                 # Pobieramy godziny pracy sklepu z konfiguracji 
235:                 hours = self.shop_config.get_open_hours_for_day(day) 
236:                 if not hours: 
237:                     continue 
238:                 shop_open_str, shop_close_str = hours 
239:                  
240:                 try: 
241:                     shop_open_h = int(shop_open_str.split(":")[0]) 
242:                     shop_close_h = int(shop_close_str.split(":")[0]) 
243:                 except: 
244:                     continue 
245:                 for emp in self.schedule.employees: 
246:                     ds = self.schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
247:                      
248:                     # Ignorujemy osoby, które nie pracują, są na urlopie lub L4 
249:                     if not ds.start or ds.is_leave or getattr(ds, "is_sick", False): 
250:                         continue 
251:                     try: 
252:                         emp_start_h = int(ds.start.split(":")[0]) 
253:                         emp_end_h = int(ds.end.split(":")[0]) 
254:                         # --- LOGIKA ZGODNA Z GENERATOREM --- 
255:                          
256:                         # 1. Dokładne Otwarcie/Zamknięcie (walidacja) 
257:                         if ds.start == shop_open_str: 
258:                             at_opening += 1 
259:                         if ds.end == shop_close_str: 
260:                             at_closing += 1 
261:                         # 2. Rano / Popołudnie (wszystkie zmiany rano i po południu) 
262:                         # Rano: Zaczyna tak jak sklep LUB przed 12:00 
263:                         if emp_start_h <= shop_open_h or emp_start_h < 12: 
264:                             total_morning += 1 
265:                          
266:                         # Popołudnie: Kończy tak jak sklep LUB zaczyna od 12:00 wzwyż 
267:                         if emp_end_h >= shop_close_h or emp_start_h >= 12: 
268:                             total_afternoon += 1 
269:                         # 3. Mięso 
270:                         if emp.is_meat and ds.start and not ds.is_leave and not getattr(ds, "is_sick", False): 
271:                             count_meat += 1 
272:                     except: 
273:                         continue 
274:                 # Budowanie tekstu do wyświetlenia 
275:                 if key == "open": 
276:                     display_text = str(at_opening) 
277:                 elif key == "close": 
278:                     display_text = str(at_closing) 
279:                 elif key == "morning_afternoon": 
280:                     # Wyświetlamy w formacie Rano / Popo 
281:                     display_text = f"{total_morning}  /  {total_afternoon}" 
282:                 elif key == "meat": 
283:                     view = constraint_presenter.get_validation_cell_view("meat", day) 
284:                     display_text = "❌" if view.bg == theme.ERR_RED else "✅" 
285:                 item = QTableWidgetItem(display_text) 
286:                 item.setTextAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter) 
287:                 # Kolorowanie tła 
288:                 if key == "morning_afternoon": 
289:                     # Wiersz informacyjny ma stały kolor panelu (taki jak prawidłowe otw/zam) 
290:                     item.setBackground(QBrush(QColor(theme.BG_PANEL))) 
291:                 else: 
292:                     # Reszta wierszy używa walidatora błędów 
293:                     view = constraint_presenter.get_validation_cell_view(key, day) 
294:                     item.setBackground(QBrush(QColor(view.bg))) 
295:                     if view.tooltip: 
296:                         item.setToolTip(view.tooltip) 
297:                 self.setItem(row, day, item) 
298:             # Wypełnienie komórek sumarycznych (ostatnie 4 kolumny) szarym kolorem 
299:             for col in range(days + 1, days + 5): 
300:                 filler = QTableWidgetItem("") 
301:                 filler.setBackground(QBrush(QColor(theme.BG_PANEL))) 
302:                 self.setItem(row, col, filler) 
303:     def paintEvent(self, event): 
304:         super().paintEvent(event) 
305:         painter = QPainter(self.viewport()) 
306:         painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing) 
307:         # 1. Obramówka aktywnego wiersza (grubsza) 
308:         if self.active_row is not None: 
309:             pen = QPen(QColor(0, 120, 215), 2) 
310:             painter.setPen(pen) 
311:             rect = self._get_full_row_rect(self.active_row) 
312:             painter.drawRect(rect.adjusted(1, 1, -1, -1)) 
313:         # 1. Obramówka najechanego wiersza (cieńsza, subtelniejsza) 
314:         if self.hovered_row is not None and self.hovered_row  
315:             pen = QPen(QColor(0, 120, 215, 80), 1) 
316:             painter.setPen(pen) 
317:             rect = self._get_full_row_rect(self.hovered_row) 
318:             painter.drawRect(rect.adjusted(1, 1, -1, -1)) 
319:     def _get_full_row_rect(self, row): 
320:         rect = self.visualRect(self.model().index(row, 0)) 
321:         for col in range(1, self.columnCount()): 
322:             rect = rect.united(self.visualRect(self.model().index(row, col))) 
323:         return rect 
324:     def _handle_click(self, row, col): 
325:         self.active_row = row 
326:         self.viewport().update() 
327:         if not self.schedule or not self.main_window: 
328:             return 
329:         emp_count = len(self.schedule.employees) 
330:         days = self.schedule.days_in_month 
331:         if row < emp_count and 1 <= col <= days: 
332:             if ( 
333:                 self.main_window.quick_mode_enabled 
334:                 and self.main_window.quick_selected_shift 
335:             ): 
336:                 self._apply_quick_shift(row, col) 
337:     def _handle_double_click(self, row, col): 
338:         if not self.schedule or not self.main_window: 
339:             return 
340:         emp_count = len(self.schedule.employees) 
341:         days = self.schedule.days_in_month 
342:         if row < emp_count and col == 0 and self.on_edit_employee: 
343:             self.on_edit_employee(self.schedule.employees[row]) 
344:             return 
345:         if row < emp_count and 1 <= col <= days: 
346:             if ( 
347:                 self.main_window.quick_mode_enabled 
348:                 and self.main_window.quick_selected_shift 
349:             ): 
350:                 self._apply_quick_shift(row, col) 
351:             elif self.on_edit_day: 
352:                 self.on_edit_day(self.schedule.employees[row], col) 
353:     def _handle_header_double_click(self, col): 
354:         # 4. Dwuklik na nagłówku 
355:         if 1 <= col <= self.schedule.days_in_month: 
356:             if self.on_header_menu: 
357:                 self.on_header_menu(col, None) 
358:     def contextMenuEvent(self, event): 
359:         # 100% pewna metoda na wyłapanie prawego przycisku myszy w Qt 
360:         global_pos = event.globalPos() 
361:          
362:         # Mapujemy globalną pozycję myszy na obszar viewportu tabeli 
363:         vp_pos = self.viewport().mapFromGlobal(global_pos) 
364:         row = self.rowAt(vp_pos.y()) 
365:         col = self.columnAt(vp_pos.x()) 
366:          
367:         if row == -1 or col == -1: 
368:             return 
369:              
370:         emp_count = len(self.schedule.employees) 
371:         days = self.schedule.days_in_month 
372:         if row >= emp_count or not (1 <= col <= days): 
373:             return 
374:         emp = self.schedule.employees[row] 
375:         day = col 
376:         ds = self.schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
377:          
378:         menu = QMenu(self) 
379:          
380:         act_copy = menu.addAction("Kopiuj dzień") 
381:         act_paste = menu.addAction("Wklej dzień") 
382:         act_paste.setEnabled(self._clipboard_day is not None) 
383:          
384:         menu.addSeparator() 
385:          
386:         act_unlock = menu.addAction("Odblokuj") 
387:         act_unlock.setEnabled(getattr(ds, "is_locked", False)) 
388:         act_clear = menu.addAction("Wyczyść komórkę") 
389:         action = menu.exec(global_pos) 
390:          
391:         if action == act_copy: 
392:             self._clipboard_day = { 
393:                 "start": ds.start, "end": ds.end,  
394:                 "is_leave": ds.is_leave, "is_sick": getattr(ds, "is_sick", False) 
395:             } 
396:             if self.main_window: 
397:                 self.main_window.statusBar().showMessage("Skopiowano dzień.", 2000) 
398:         elif action == act_paste: 
399:             self.controller.set_day_hours(emp, day, self._clipboard_day["start"], self._clipboard_day["end"]) 
400:             self.refresh() 
401:         elif action == act_unlock: 
402:             self.controller.snapshot() # Ręczny zapis przed manipulacją obiektem 
403:             ds.is_locked = False 
404:             self.refresh() 
405:         elif action == act_clear: 
406:             self.controller.snapshot() 
407:             ds.start = None 
408:             ds.end = None 
409:             ds.is_leave = False 
410:             ds.is_sick = False 
411:             ds.is_locked = False 
412:             self.refresh() 
413:     def _open_header_context_menu(self, pos): 
414:         # Zachowuję sygnaturę z Twojego oryginalnego kodu (teraz zastąpione przez double_click) 
415:         pass 
416:     def _apply_quick_shift(self, row, col): 
417:         if not self.main_window: 
418:             return 
419:         emp = self.schedule.employees[row] 
420:         day = col 
421:         shift = self.main_window.quick_selected_shift 
422:         start = None 
423:         end = None 
424:         if shift == "WORK": 
425:             start = self.main_window.start_input.get_time_str() 
426:             end = self.main_window.end_input.get_time_str() 
427:             from datetime import datetime 
428:             fmt = "%H:%M" 
429:             try: 
430:                 start_dt = datetime.strptime(start, fmt) 
431:                 end_dt = datetime.strptime(end, fmt) 
432:             except: 
433:                 return 
434:             if end_dt <= start_dt: 
435:                 from PySide6.QtWidgets import QMessageBox 
436:                 QMessageBox.warning(self, "Błąd", "Godzina zakończenia musi być późniejsza niż rozpoczęcia.") 
437:                 return 
438:         elif shift == "SICK": 
439:             self.controller.snapshot() # Zabezpieczenie undo 
440:             ds = self.schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
441:             ds.is_locked = False 
442:             self.controller.set_day_sick(emp, day) 
443:             self.refresh() 
444:             return 
445:         elif shift == "OFF": 
446:             ds = self.schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
447:             # 🔥 jeśli już jest wolne → nic nie rób 
448:             if getattr(ds, "is_locked", False) and not ds.start and not ds.end and not ds.is_leave and not getattr(ds, "is_sick", False): 
449:                 return 
450:             self.controller.snapshot() # Zabezpieczenie undo 
451:             # 🔥 ustaw wolne ręczne 
452:             ds.start = None 
453:             ds.end = None 
454:             ds.is_leave = False 
455:             ds.is_sick = False 
456:             ds.is_locked = True 
457:             self.refresh() 
458:             return 
459:         ds = self.schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
460:         current = ( 
461:             ds.start, 
462:             ds.end, 
463:             ds.is_leave, 
464:             getattr(ds, "is_sick", False) 
465:         ) 
466:         new = (start, end, shift == "LEAVE", shift == "SICK") 
467:         if current == new: 
468:             return 
469:         self.controller.snapshot() # Zabezpieczenie undo 
470:         ds.is_locked = False 
471:         self.controller.set_shift(emp, day, shift, start, end) 
472:         self.refresh() 
473:     def set_clipboard(self, value): 
474:         self._clipboard_day = value 
475:     def clipboard(self): 
476:         return self._clipboard_day 
477:     def set_compact_mode(self, enabled: bool): 
478:         self.compact_mode = enabled 
479:         self.refresh() 
480:     def clear_schedule(self): 
481:         if not self.schedule or not self.controller: 
482:             return 
483:         from PySide6.QtWidgets import QMessageBox 
484:         reply = QMessageBox.question( 
485:             self, 
486:             "Potwierdzenie", 
487:             "Czy na pewno chcesz skasować wszystkie dane na grafiku?", 
488:             QMessageBox.Yes | QMessageBox.No, 
489:             QMessageBox.No 
490:         ) 
491:         if reply  
492:             return 
493:         self.controller.snapshot() 
494:         for emp in self.schedule.employees: 
495:             for day in range(1, self.schedule.days_in_month + 1): 
496:                 ds = self.schedule.get_day(emp, day) 
497:                 ds.start = None 
498:                 ds.end = None 
499:                 ds.is_leave = False 
500:                 ds.is_sick = False 
501:                 ds.is_locked = False 
502:         self.refresh()

1: import os 
2: import json 
3: import uuid 
4: import hashlib 
5: from PySide6.QtWidgets import QInputDialog, QMessageBox 
6: LICENSE_FILE = "license.json" 
7: def get_user_id() -> str: 
8:     raw = str(uuid.getnode()) 
9:     hash_hex = hashlib.sha256(raw.encode()).hexdigest() 
10:     return hash_hex[:8].upper() 
11: def validate_license(user_id: str, key: str) -> bool: 
12:     secret = "dupadupa" 
13:     raw = user_id + secret 
14:     hash_hex = hashlib.sha256(raw.encode()).hexdigest() 
15:     digits = ''.join(filter(str.isdigit, hash_hex)) 
16:     expected = digits[:8] 
17:     return key == expected 
18: def save_license(key: str): 
19:     with open(LICENSE_FILE, "w") as f: 
20:         json.dump({"key": key}, f) 
21: def load_license(): 
22:     if not os.path.exists(LICENSE_FILE): 
23:         return None 
24:     try: 
25:         with open(LICENSE_FILE, "r") as f: 
26:             data = json.load(f) 
27:             return data.get("key") 
28:     except: 
29:         return None 
30: def show_license_dialog(parent): 
31:     text, ok = QInputDialog.getText( 
32:         parent, 
33:         "Aktywacja produktu", 
34:         "Podaj klucz produktu:" 
35:     ) 
36:     if not ok or not text: 
37:         return 
38:     user_id = get_user_id() 
39:     if validate_license(user_id, text): 
40:         save_license(text) 
41:         parent.demo.is_demo = False 
42:         QMessageBox.information(parent, "Sukces", "Program aktywowany") 
43:     else: 
44:         QMessageBox.warning(parent, "Błąd", "Niepoprawny klucz.")

1: from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QTimer 
2: from PySide6.QtGui import QPainter, QColor

4: class LoadingSpinner(QWidget): 
5:     def __init__(self, parent=None): 
6:         super().__init__(parent) 
7:         self._angle = 0 
8:         self._timer = QTimer(self) 
9:         self._timer.timeout.connect(self._rotate) 
10:         self._timer.start(16) 
11:         self.setFixedSize(80, 80) 
12:     def _rotate(self): 
13:         self._angle = (self._angle + 5) % 360 
14:         self.update() 
15:     def paintEvent(self, event): 
16:         painter = QPainter(self) 
17:         painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing) 
18:         rect = self.rect().adjusted(10, 10, -10, -10) 
19:         painter.setPen(QColor(255, 255, 255, 40)) 
20:         painter.drawEllipse(rect) 
21:         pen = painter.pen() 
22:         pen.setWidth(4) 
23:         pen.setColor(QColor(255, 255, 255)) 
24:         painter.setPen(pen) 
25:         painter.drawArc(rect, int(self._angle * 16), int(120 * 16)) 
26: class LoadingOverlay(QWidget):

52:         ]

54:         self.timer = QTimer(self) 
55:         self.timer.timeout.connect(self._next_message) 
56:     def _next_message(self): 
57:         self.label.setText(self.messages[self._msg_index]) 
58:         self._msg_index = (self._msg_index + 1) % len(self.messages) 
59:     def show_overlay(self): 
60:         self.setGeometry(0, 0, self.parent().width(), self.parent().height()) 
61:         self.raise_() 
62:         self._msg_index = 0 
63:         self.label.setText(self.messages[0]) 
64:         self.show() 
65:         self.timer.start(3000) 
66:     def hide_overlay(self): 
67:         self.timer.stop()

361:     def _init_state(self): 
362:         old_employees = [] 
363:         if self.schedule: 
364:             old_employees = self.schedule.employees 
365:         self.schedule = MonthSchedule(self.year, self.month, employees=old_employees) 
366:         self.shop_config = ShopConfig(self.year, self.month) 
367:         self.controller = ScheduleController(self.schedule, self.shop_config) 
368:     def _sync_everything(self): 
369:         self._sync_grid() 
370:         self._update_window_title() 
371:         self._update_state_label() 
372:         self._update_generate_label() 
373:         if getattr(self.schedule, "is_generated", False): 
374:             self.btn_generate.setText("Napraw grafik") 
375:             self.btn_generate.setToolTip("Naprawia istniejący grafik bez pełnej regeneracji") 
376:             self.btn_regenerate.show() 
377:             self.btn_regenerate.setToolTip("Wymusza pełne generowanie od zera. Nadpisuje cały grafik") 
378:         else: 
379:             self.btn_generate.setText("Generuj grafik") 
380:             self.btn_generate.setToolTip("Tworzy nowy grafik od zera. Nie nadpisuje ręcznie wprowadzonych zmian") 
381:             self.btn_regenerate.hide() 
382:     def _sync_grid(self): 
383:         self.grid.set_data( 
384:             self.schedule, 
385:             self.shop_config, 
386:             self.controller, 
387:             main_window=self, 
388:             on_edit_day=self._edit_day, 
389:             on_edit_employee=self._edit_employee, 
390:             on_context_menu=self._open_day_context_menu, 
391:             on_header_menu=self._open_header_menu, 
392:         ) 
393:         self.grid.build() 
394:         self.grid.refresh() 
395:     def _update_window_title(self): 
396:         self.setWindowTitle(f"Grafik Dino — {self.month:02d}.{self.year}") 
397:     def _update_nominal_hours_label(self): 
398:         if not self.shop_config: 
399:             self.nominal_hours_label.setText("-") 
400:             return 
401:         hours = self.shop_config.get_full_time_nominal_hours() 
402:         self.nominal_hours_label.setText(f"{hours} h") 
403:     def _update_state_label(self): 
404:         if not self.schedule: 
405:             self.state_label.setText("") 
406:             return 
407:         self.state_label.setText(f"{self.month:02d}.{self.year}") 
408:     def _set_date_controls(self, year, month): 
409:         self._loading = True 
410:         self.year_spin.blockSignals(True) 
411:         self.month_spin.blockSignals(True) 
412:         self.year_spin.setValue(year) 
413:         self.month_spin.setValue(month) 
414:         if hasattr(self, 'date_display_label'): 
415:             self.date_display_label.setText(f"{month:02d}.{year}") 
416:         self.year_spin.blockSignals(False) 
417:         self.month_spin.blockSignals(False) 
418:         self._loading = False 
419:     def _enter_edit_date_mode(self): 
420:         self.date_display_label.hide() 
421:         self.btn_change_date.hide() 
422:         self.date_edit_widget.show() 
423:     def _save_date_clicked(self): 
424:         new_year = self.year_spin.value() 
425:         new_month = self.month_spin.value() 
426:          
427:         if new_year == self.year and new_month == self.month: 
428:             self.date_edit_widget.hide() 
429:             self.date_display_label.show() 
430:             self.btn_change_date.show() 
431:             return 
432:         msg_box = QMessageBox(self) 
433:         msg_box.setWindowTitle("Zmiana miesiąca") 
434:         msg_box.setText("Zmiana miesiąca spowoduje usunięcie wszystkich zmian wprowadzonych na grafiku. Kontynuować?") 
435:         btn_yes = msg_box.addButton("Tak", QMessageBox.YesRole) 
436:         btn_cancel = msg_box.addButton("Anuluj", QMessageBox.RejectRole) 
437:         msg_box.exec() 
438:         if msg_box.clickedButton() == btn_yes: 
439:             self._loading = True 
440:             self.year = new_year 
441:             self.month = new_month 
442:             self.date_display_label.setText(f"{self.month:02d}.{self.year}") 
443:              
444:             self._init_state() 
445:             self._update_nominal_hours_label() 
446:             self._sync_everything() 
447:             self.statusBar().showMessage("Utworzono nowy grafik dla wybranego miesiąca.", 2500) 
448:              
449:             self.date_edit_widget.hide() 
450:             self.date_display_label.show() 
451:             self.btn_change_date.show() 
452:             self._loading = False 
453:         else: 
454:             self._set_date_controls(self.year, self.month) 
455:             self.date_edit_widget.hide() 
456:             self.date_display_label.show() 
457:             self.btn_change_date.show() 
458:     def _on_generate_clicked(self): 
459:         self._generate_schedule(force=False) 
460:          
461:     def _on_force_generate_clicked(self): 
462:         self._generate_schedule(force=True) 
463:     def _generate_schedule(self, force=False): 
464:         if not self.demo.can_generate(self): 
465:             return 
466:         if not self.schedule.employees: 
467:             QMessageBox.information(self, "Generowanie", "Dodaj pracowników przed generowaniem grafiku.") 
468:             return 
469:         self._show_loading() 
470:         self.thread = QThread() 
471:         self.worker = GeneratorWorker(self.controller, force=force) 
472:         self.worker.moveToThread(self.thread) 
473:         self.thread.started.connect(self.worker.run) 
474:         self.worker.finished.connect(self._on_generation_finished) 
475:         self.worker.finished.connect(self.thread.quit) 
476:         self.worker.finished.connect(self.worker.deleteLater) 
477:         self.thread.finished.connect(self.thread.deleteLater) 
478:         self.thread.start() 
479:     def _on_generation_finished(self, result): 
480:         self._hide_loading() 
481:         self.schedule = self.controller.schedule 
482:         self._sync_everything() 
483:         if result and result.get("success"): 
484:             self.demo.register_generation() 
485:             self._update_generate_label() 
486:             if self.demo.is_demo: 
487:                 self.demo.show_after_generate(self) 
488:             else: 
489:                 QMessageBox.information(self, "Sukces", "Grafik został wygenerowany.") 
490:         else: 
491:             QMessageBox.warning(self, "Brak rozwiązania", "Nie udało się znaleźć poprawnego grafiku.") 
492:     def _open_add_employee(self): 
493:         dialog = EmployeeDialog(self) 
494:         if dialog.exec()  
495:             return 
496:         self.controller.add_employee(dialog.employee_result) 
497:         self.schedule = self.controller.schedule 
498:         self._sync_everything() 
499:         self.statusBar().showMessage("Dodano pracownika.", 2500) 
500:     def _edit_employee(self, emp): 
501:         dialog = EmployeeDialog(self, employee=emp) 
502:         if dialog.exec()  
503:             return 
504:         if dialog.employee_result is None: 
505:             self.controller.remove_employee(emp) 
506:             self.schedule = self.controller.schedule 
507:             self._sync_everything() 
508:             self.statusBar().showMessage("Usunięto pracownika.", 2500) 
509:             return 
510:         self.controller.replace_employee(emp, dialog.employee_result) 
511:         self.schedule = self.controller.schedule 
512:         self._sync_everything() 
513:         self.statusBar().showMessage("Zapisano pracownika.", 2500) 
514:     def _edit_day(self, emp, day): 
515:         hours = self.shop_config.get_open_hours_for_day(day) 
516:         if not hours: 
517:             return 
518:         ds = self.controller.get_day(emp, day) 
519:         dialog = DayEditDialog( 
520:             self, 
521:             start=None if ds.is_leave else ds.start, 
522:             end=None if ds.is_leave else ds.end, 
523:             open_start=hours[0], 
524:             open_end=hours[1], 
525:             daily_hours=emp.daily_hours, 
526:         ) 
527:         if dialog.exec()  
528:             return 
529:         if dialog.result_mode == "free": 
530:             self.controller.set_day_free(emp, day) 
531:         elif dialog.result_mode == "leave": 
532:             self.controller.set_day_leave(emp, day) 
533:         elif dialog.result_mode == "sick": 
534:             self.controller.set_day_sick(emp, day) 
535:         elif dialog.result_mode == "hours": 
536:             from datetime import datetime 
537:             fmt = "%H:%M" 
538:             try: 
539:                 start_dt = datetime.strptime(dialog.result_start, fmt) 
540:                 end_dt = datetime.strptime(dialog.result_end, fmt) 
541:             except: 
542:                 QMessageBox.warning(self, "Błąd", "Niepoprawny format godziny.") 
543:                 return 
544:             if end_dt <= start_dt: 
545:                 QMessageBox.warning(self, "Błąd", "Godzina zakończenia musi być późniejsza niż rozpoczęcia.") 
546:                 return 
547:             self.controller.set_day_hours(emp, day, dialog.result_start, dialog.result_end) 
548:         self.schedule = self.controller.schedule 
549:         self._sync_everything() 
550:         self.statusBar().showMessage("Zmieniono dzień.", 2500) 
551:     def _open_day_context_menu(self, emp, day, global_pos): 
552:         if not self.shop_config.is_trade_day(day): 
553:             return 
554:         menu = QMenu(self) 
555:         menu.addAction("Rano", lambda: self._ctx_morning(emp, day)) 
556:         menu.addAction("Zamknięcie", lambda: self._ctx_close(emp, day)) 
557:         menu.addSeparator() 
558:         menu.addAction("Kopiuj dzień", lambda: self._ctx_copy(emp, day)) 
559:         menu.addAction("Wklej dzień", lambda: self._ctx_paste(emp, day)) 
560:         menu.addSeparator() 
561:         menu.addAction("Ustaw wolne", lambda: self._ctx_free(emp, day)) 
562:         menu.addAction("Ustaw urlop", lambda: self._ctx_leave(emp, day)) 
563:         menu.exec(global_pos) 
564:     def _ctx_copy(self, emp, day): 
565:         ds = self.controller.get_day(emp, day) 
566:         if ds.is_empty(): 
567:             self._clipboard_day = None 
568:             self.grid.set_clipboard(None) 
569:             return 
570:         self._clipboard_day = { 
571:             "start": ds.start, 
572:             "end": ds.end, 
573:             "is_leave": ds.is_leave, 
574:         } 
575:         self.grid.set_clipboard(self._clipboard_day) 
576:         self.statusBar().showMessage("Skopiowano dzień.", 1800) 
577:     def _ctx_paste(self, emp, day): 
578:         clip = self.grid.clipboard() or self._clipboard_day 
579:         if not clip: 
580:             return 
581:         if clip.get("is_leave"): 
582:             self.controller.set_day_leave(emp, day) 
583:         else: 
584:             self.controller.set_day_hours(emp, day, clip["start"], clip["end"]) 
585:         self.schedule = self.controller.schedule 
586:         self._sync_everything() 
587:     def _ctx_free(self, emp, day): 
588:         self.controller.set_day_free(emp, day) 
589:         self.schedule = self.controller.schedule 
590:         self._sync_everything() 
591:     def _ctx_leave(self, emp, day): 
592:         self.controller.set_day_leave(emp, day) 
593:         self.schedule = self.controller.schedule 
594:         self._sync_everything() 
595:     def _ctx_sick(self, emp, day): 
596:         self.controller.set_day_sick(emp, day) 
597:         self.schedule = self.controller.schedule 
598:         self._sync_everything() 
599:     def _ctx_morning(self, emp, day): 
600:         hours = self.shop_config.get_open_hours_for_day(day) 
601:         if not hours: 
602:             return 
603:         start = hours[0] 
604:         end = self._calc_end_from_daily(start, emp.daily_hours) 
605:         self.controller.set_day_hours(emp, day, start, end) 
606:         self.schedule = self.controller.schedule 
607:         self._sync_everything() 
608:     def _ctx_close(self, emp, day): 
609:         hours = self.shop_config.get_open_hours_for_day(day) 
610:         if not hours: 
611:             return 
612:         end = hours[1] 
613:         start = self._calc_start_from_daily(end, emp.daily_hours) 
614:         self.controller.set_day_hours(emp, day, start, end) 
615:         self.schedule = self.controller.schedule 
616:         self._sync_everything() 
617:     def _open_header_menu(self, day, global_pos): 
618:         hours = self.shop_config.get_open_hours_for_day(day) 
619:         if not hours: 
620:             weekday = self.shop_config.weekday(day) 
621:             hours = self.shop_config.get_open_hours_for_weekday(weekday) 
622:         dialog = DayOverrideDialog(self, day, hours, self.shop_config) 
623:         result = dialog.exec() 
624:         if result  
625:             return 
626:         if dialog.result_mode == "reset":

631:             if dialog.result_holiday: 
632:                 self.shop_config.public_holidays.add(day) 
633:             else: 
634:                 self.shop_config.public_holidays.discard(day)

636:         self._sync_grid()

691:     def _print_schedule(self): 
692:         if self.demo.block_export(self): 
693:             return 
694:         if not self.schedule: 
695:             QMessageBox.warning(self, "Drukowanie", "Brak grafiku do wydruku.") 
696:             return 
697:         try: 
698:             import tempfile 
699:             from PySide6.QtGui import QPageLayout 
700:             # 1. generujemy obraz 
701:             temp_file = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(delete=False, suffix=".jpg") 
702:             temp_path = temp_file.name 
703:             temp_file.close() 
704:             export_schedule_to_image(self.schedule, self.year, self.month, temp_path) 
705:             # 2. printer 
706:             printer = QPrinter(QPrinter.HighResolution) 
707:             printer.setPageOrientation(QPageLayout.Landscape) 
708:             # 3. preview (czysty — bez styli) 
709:             preview = QPrintPreviewDialog(printer, self) 
710:             preview.setStyleSheet("") 
711:             # 4. render 
712:             def render(printer): 
713:                 painter = QPainter(printer) 
714:                 image = QImage(temp_path) 
715:                 if image.isNull(): 
716:                     painter.end() 
717:                     return 
718:                 rect = painter.viewport() 
719:                 size = image.size() 
720:                 size.scale(rect.size(), Qt.KeepAspectRatio) 
721:                 painter.setViewport(rect.x(), rect.y(), size.width(), size.height()) 
722:                 painter.setWindow(image.rect()) 
723:                 painter.drawImage(0, 0, image) 
724:                 painter.end() 
725:             preview.paintRequested.connect(render) 
726:             # 5. odpal preview 
727:             preview.exec() 
728:         except Exception as e: 
729:             QMessageBox.critical(self, "Błąd drukowania", str(e))

866:     def _quick_suggest_end(self): 
867:         from datetime import datetime, timedelta 
868:         fmt = "%H:%M" 
869:         try: 
870:             start = datetime.strptime(self.start_input.get_time_str(), fmt) 
871:         except: 
872:             return 
873:         hours = 8  # możesz potem podpiąć pod pracownika 
874:         end = start + timedelta(hours=hours) 
875:         self.end_input.input.blockSignals(True) 
876:         self.end_input.set_time_str(end.strftime(fmt)) 
877:         self.end_input.input.blockSignals(False) 
878:     def _quick_update_duration(self): 
879:         from datetime import datetime 
880:         fmt = "%H:%M" 
881:         try: 
882:             start = datetime.strptime(self.start_input.get_time_str(), fmt) 
883:             end = datetime.strptime(self.end_input.get_time_str(), fmt) 
884:         except: 
885:             self.quick_duration_label.setText(f"Czas pracy: {h}:{m:02d}") 
886:             return 
887:         minutes = int((end - start).total_seconds() / 60) 
888:         if minutes < 0: 
889:             minutes = 0 
890:         h = minutes // 60 
891:         m = minutes % 60 
892:         self.quick_duration_label.setText(f"Czas pracy: {h}:{m:02d}")

905:                 event.ignore() 
906:                 return 
907:             try: 
908:                 save_project("last_project.json", self.schedule, self.shop_config) 
909:             except:

911:             event.accept() 
912:         elif clicked == btn_no: 
913:             event.accept()

1: from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QLineEdit 
2: from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QEvent 
3: class TimeInputWidget(QWidget): 
4:     def __init__(self, parent=None): 
5:         super().__init__(parent) 
6:         layout = QHBoxLayout(self) 
7:         layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0) 
8:         layout.setSpacing(4) 
9:         layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter) 
10:         self.input = QLineEdit() 
11:         self.input.setMaxLength(5) 
12:         self.input.setFixedWidth(70) 
13:         self.input.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter) 
14:         self.input.setStyleSheet(""" 
15:             QLineEdit { 
16:                 border: 1px solid #ccc; 
17:                 border-radius: 4px; 
18:                 background: white; 
19:                 padding: 2px; 
20:                 font-size: 14px; 
21:                 selection-background-color: #0078d4; 
22:                 selection-color: white; 
23:             } 
24:             QLineEdit:focus { 
25:                 border: 2px solid #0078d4; 
26:                 background-color: #f0f7ff; 
27:             } 
28:         """) 
29:         self.input.textChanged.connect(self._format_time) 
30:         # 🔥 focus czyści pole 
31:         self.input.installEventFilter(self) 
32:         layout.addWidget(self.input) 
33:         self.set_time_str("00:00") 
34:     def eventFilter(self, obj, event): 
35:         if obj == self.input and event.type() == QEvent.FocusIn: 
36:             self.input.blockSignals(True) 
37:             self.input.clear() 
38:             self.input.blockSignals(False) 
39:         return super().eventFilter(obj, event) 
40:     def _format_time(self, text): 
41:         digits = "".join(filter(str.isdigit, text)) 
42:         # 🔥 auto 0 jeśli pierwsza cyfra > 2 
43:         if len(digits) == 1 and int(digits[0]) > 2: 
44:             digits = "0" + digits 
45:         digits = digits[:4] 
46:         if len(digits) >= 3: 
47:             formatted = f"{digits[:2]}:{digits[2:]}" 
48:         else: 
49:             formatted = digits 
50:         if formatted  
51:             self.input.blockSignals(True) 
52:             self.input.setText(formatted) 
53:             self.input.blockSignals(False) 
54:     def get_time_str(self): 
55:         text = self.input.text() 
56:         if len(text) == 5 and ":" in text: 
57:             return text 
58:         return "00:00" 
59:     def set_time_str(self, time_str): 
60:         if not time_str or ":" not in time_str: 
61:             self.input.setText("00:00") 
62:             return 
63:         try: 
64:             h, m = map(int, time_str.split(":")) 
65:             self.input.setText(f"{h:02d}:{m:02d}") 
66:         except ValueError: 
67:             self.input.setText("00:00")

FILE: C:\Users\kewi1\Desktop\Grafik dino V2\ui\tooltip.py 
================================================== 
 
1: import tkinter as tk 
2: class Tooltip: 
3:     def __init__(self, widget, text: str): 
4:         self.widget = widget 
5:         self.text = text 
6:         self.tip = None 
7:         # zapamiętaj ID bindingów 
8:         self._enter_id = widget.bind("<Enter>", self.show) 
9:         self._leave_id = widget.bind("<Leave>", self.hide) 
10:     def show(self, _=None): 
11:         if self.tip or not self.text: 
12:             return 
13:         x = self.widget.winfo_rootx() + 15 
14:         y = self.widget.winfo_rooty() + 15 
15:         self.tip = tk.Toplevel(self.widget) 
16:         self.tip.wm_overrideredirect(True) 
17:         self.tip.wm_geometry(f"+{x}+{y}") 
18:         tk.Label( 
19:             self.tip, 
20:             text=self.text, 
21:             background="#ffffe0", 
22:             relief="solid", 
23:             borderwidth=1, 
24:             font=("Arial", 9) 
25:         ).pack() 
26:     def hide(self, _=None): 
27:         if self.tip: 
28:             self.tip.destroy() 
29:             self.tip = None 
30:     def destroy(self): 
31:         # usuń tooltip jeśli widoczny 
32:         self.hide() 
33:         # odłącz bindingi 
34:         if self._enter_id: 
35:             self.widget.unbind("<Enter>", self._enter_id) 
36:         if self._leave_id: 
37:             self.widget.unbind("<Leave>", self._leave_id)
